  \begin{theorem}{frobdefect-row-testbit-eq-of-rowagree}
  \allowaxioms{Classical.choice;Quot.sound;propext}
  For every natural number \(t\) and natural number \(k\) and natural number \(Q\) and natural number \(a\) and natural number \(L\) and natural number \(i\), if \(twopow(succ(k)) ≤ a\), then if \(i ≤ L\), then if \(rowagree(t, Q, a - twopow(succ(k)), L + 1)\), then if \(rowagree(t, Q, a - twopow(k), L + 1)\), then if \(rowagree(t, Q, a, L + 1)\), then \(bitfrobdefecteq(k, wittrow(t + Q), a + i + 1 + Q, wittbitbool(frobdefect(k, wittrow(t)), a + i + 1))\).
  \begin{proof}
  Close the goal with \(\lexeme{r30.witt::frobdefect-row-testbit-eq-of-rowagree}(t, k, Q, a, L, i)\).
  \end{proof}
  \end{theorem}

  \begin{theorem}{crosscarry-testbit-eq-of-rowagree-winwittagree}
  \allowaxioms{Quot.sound;propext}
  For every natural number \(t\) and natural number \(k\) and natural number \(Q\) and natural number \(a\) and natural number \(L\) and natural number \(i\), if \(twopow(succ(k)) ≤ a\), then if \(i ≤ L\), then if \(winwittagree(t, k, Q, a, L + 1)\), then if \(rowagree(t, Q, a - twopow(succ(k)), L + 1)\), then if \(rowagree(t, Q, a - twopow(k), L + 1)\), then if \(rowagree(t, Q, a, L + 1)\), then \(bitcrosscarryeq(wittscale(k, wittrow(t + Q)), wittwin(t + Q, twopow(k)), a + i + 1 + Q, wittbitbool(crosscarry(wittscale(k, wittrow(t)), wittwin(t, twopow(k))), a + i + 1))\).
  \begin{proof}
  Close the goal with \(\lexeme{r30.witt::crosscarry-testbit-eq-of-rowagree-winwittagree}(t, k, Q, a, L, i)\).
  \end{proof}
  \end{theorem}

  \begin{theorem}{winwittagree-step}
  \allowaxioms{Classical.choice;Quot.sound;propext}
  For every natural number \(t\) and natural number \(k\) and natural number \(Q\) and natural number \(a\) and natural number \(L\), if \(twopow(succ(k)) ≤ a\), then if \(winwittagree(t, k, Q, a, L + 2)\), then if \(rowagree(t, Q, a - twopow(succ(k)), L + 1)\), then if \(rowagree(t, Q, a - twopow(k), L + 1)\), then if \(rowagree(t, Q, a, L + 1)\), then \(winwittagree(t + 1, k, Q, a + 2, L)\).
  \begin{proof}
  Close the goal with \(\lexeme{r30.witt::winwittagree-step}(t, k, Q, a, L)\).
  \end{proof}
  \end{theorem}

  \begin{theorem}{winwittagree-steps}
  \allowaxioms{Classical.choice;Quot.sound;propext}
  For every natural number \(t\) and natural number \(k\) and natural number \(Q\) and natural number \(a\) and natural number \(L\) and natural number \(s\), if \(twopow(succ(k)) ≤ a\), then if \(winwittagree(t, k, Q, a, L + 2 * s)\), then if \(rowagree(t, Q, a - twopow(succ(k)), L + 2 * s)\), then if \(rowagree(t, Q, a - twopow(k), L + 2 * s)\), then if \(rowagree(t, Q, a, L + 2 * s)\), then \(winwittagree(t + s, k, Q, a + 2 * s, L)\).
  \begin{proof}
  Close the goal with \(\lexeme{r30.witt::winwittagree-steps}(t, k, Q, a, L, s)\).
  \end{proof}
  \end{theorem}

  \begin{theorem}{wittresp-eq-of-winwittagree-steps}
  \allowaxioms{Classical.choice;Quot.sound;propext}
  For every natural number \(t0\) and natural number \(k\) and natural number \(Q\) and natural number \(s\), if \(twopow(k) + s ≤ t0\), then if \(winwittagree(t0, k, Q, t0 + twopow(k) - s, 2 * s)\), then if \(rowagree(t0, Q, t0 + twopow(k) - s - twopow(succ(k)), 2 * s)\), then if \(rowagree(t0, Q, t0 + twopow(k) - s - twopow(k), 2 * s)\), then if \(rowagree(t0, Q, t0 + twopow(k) - s, 2 * s)\), then \(wittresp(t0 + s + Q, k) = wittresp(t0 + s, k)\).
  \begin{proof}
  Close the goal with \(\lexeme{r30.witt::wittresp-eq-of-winwittagree-steps}(t0, k, Q, s)\).
  \end{proof}
  \end{theorem}

  \begin{theorem}{colm1-eq-of-rowagree-steps}
  \allowaxioms{Classical.choice;Quot.sound;propext}
  For every natural number \(t0\) and natural number \(Q\) and natural number \(s\), if \(s ≤ t0 + 2\), then if \(rowagree(t0 + 1, Q, t0 + 2 - s, 2 * s)\), then \(ldiag(t0 + s + Q, t0 + s + 1 + Q) = ldiag(t0 + s, t0 + s + 1)\).
  \begin{proof}
  Close the goal with \(\lexeme{r30.witt::colm1-eq-of-rowagree-steps}(t0, Q, s)\).
  \end{proof}
  \end{theorem}

  \allowaxioms{Classical.choice;Quot.sound;propext}
  For every natural number \(T\) and natural number \(P\), if \(0 < P\), then if \(centerperiodic(T, P)\), then for every natural number \(m\) and natural number \(t0\) and natural number \(s\), if \(T ≤ t0 + s + 1\), then if \(s ≤ t0 + 2\), then if \(rowagree(t0 + 1, m * P, t0 + 2 - s, 2 * s)\), then \(maskparity(t0 + s + 1 + m * P, 0) = maskparity(t0 + s + 1, 0)\).
  \begin{proof}
  Assume \(hP\), \(hper\).
  Close the goal with \(\lexeme{r30.witt::maskparity-zero-eq-of-rowagree-steps}(T, P, hP, hper)\).
  \end{proof}
  \end{theorem}

  \begin{theorem}{colnegoneperiodic-of-sector-rowagree}
  \allowaxioms{Classical.choice;Quot.sound;propext}
  For every natural number \(k\) and natural number \(d\) and natural number \(U\) and natural number \(Q\), if \(1 ≤ U\), then if \(sectorrowagree(k, d, U, Q)\), then \(colnegoneperiodic(d + U * twopow(k), Q)\).
  \begin{proof}

  \end{theorem}

  \allowaxioms{Classical.choice;Quot.sound;propext}
  For every natural number \(T\) and natural number \(P\), if \(0 < P\), then if \(centerperiodic(T, P)\), then for every natural number \(k\) and natural number \(d\) and natural number \(U\) and natural number \(m\), if \(1 ≤ U\), then if \(0 < m\), then not \(sectorrowagree(k, d, U, m * P)\).

  \begin{theorem}{rdiag-lcmperiod-eq}
  \allowaxioms{Classical.choice;Quot.sound;propext}
  For every natural number \(P\) and natural number \(k\) and natural number \(j\) and natural number \(t\), if \(j ≤ k\), then \(rdiag(j, t + lcmperiod(P, k)) = rdiag(j, t)\).

  \end{proof}

  \begin{theorem}{ldiag-lcmperiod-eq}
  \allowaxioms{Classical.choice;Quot.sound;propext}
  For every natural number \(P\) and natural number \(k\) and natural number \(j\) and natural number \(t\), if \(j ≤ k\), then if \(settledby(j, t)\), then \(ldiag(j, t + lcmperiod(P, k)) = ldiag(j, t)\).
  \begin{proof}
  Close the goal with \(\lexeme{r30.witt::ldiag-lcmperiod-eq}(P, k, j, t)\).
  \end{proof}
  \end{theorem}

  \begin{theorem}{center-lcmperiod-eq}
  \allowaxioms{Classical.choice;Quot.sound;propext}
  For every natural number \(T\) and natural number \(P\), if \(0 < P\), then if \(centerperiodic(T, P)\), then for every natural number \(k\) and natural number \(t\), if \(T ≤ t\), then \(center(t + lcmperiod(P, k)) = center(t)\).

  \begin{theorem}{not-sector-rowagree-lcmperiod}

  \begin{theorem}{rowagree-extend-right}
  \allowaxioms{Classical.choice;Quot.sound;propext}
  For every natural number \(t\) and natural number \(Q\) and natural number \(a\) and natural number \(L\), if \(rowagree(t, Q, a, L + 1)\), then if \(wittbitbool(wittrow((t + 1) + Q), ((a + L) + 2) + Q) = wittbitbool(wittrow(t + 1), (a + L) + 2)\), then \(rowagree(t, Q, a, L + 2)\).

  \allowaxioms{Classical.choice;Quot.sound;propext}
  For every natural number \(T\) and natural number \(P\), if \(0 < P\), then if \(centerperiodic(T, P)\), then for every natural number \(m\) and natural number \(R\) and natural number \(t\), if \(T ≤ t\), then if \(R ≤ t\), then if \(rowagree(t, m * P, t - R, R)\), then \(rowagree(t, m * P, t - R, 2 * R)\).

  \begin{theorem}{winwittagree-extend-right}
  \allowaxioms{Classical.choice;Quot.sound;propext}
  For every natural number \(t\) and natural number \(k\) and natural number \(Q\) and natural number \(a\) and natural number \(L\), if \(twopow(succ(k)) ≤ a\), then if \(winwittagree(t, k, Q, a, L + 1)\), then if \(rowagree(t, Q, a - twopow(succ(k)), L + 1)\), then if \(rowagree(t, Q, a - twopow(k), L + 1)\), then if \(rowagree(t, Q, a, L + 1)\), then if \(wittbitbool(wittwin((t + 1) + Q, twopow(k)), ((a + L) + 2) + Q) = wittbitbool(wittwin(t + 1, twopow(k)), (a + L) + 2)\), then \(winwittagree(t, k, Q, a, L + 2)\).
  \begin{proof}
  Close the goal with \(\lexeme{r30.witt::winwittagree-extend-right}(t, k, Q, a, L)\).
  \end{proof}

  \begin{theorem}{winwittagree-of-rowagree-three-and-late}
  \allowaxioms{Classical.choice;Quot.sound;propext}
  For every natural number \(t\) and natural number \(k\) and natural number \(Q\) and natural number \(a\) and natural number \(L\), if \(twopow(succ(k)) ≤ a\), then if \(rowagree(t, Q, a - twopow(succ(k)), L)\), then if \(rowagree(t, Q, a - twopow(k), L)\), then if \(rowagree(t, Q, a, L)\), then if \(rowagree(t + twopow(k), Q, a, L)\), then \(winwittagree(t, k, Q, a, L)\).
  \begin{proof}
  Close the goal with \(\lexeme{r30.witt::winwittagree-of-rowagree-three-and-late}(t, k, Q, a, L)\).

  \begin{theorem}{rowagree-late-of-winwittagree-and-three}
  \allowaxioms{Classical.choice;Quot.sound;propext}
  For every natural number \(t\) and natural number \(k\) and natural number \(Q\) and natural number \(a\) and natural number \(L\), if \(twopow(succ(k)) ≤ a\), then if \(winwittagree(t, k, Q, a, L)\), then if \(rowagree(t, Q, a - twopow(succ(k)), L)\), then if \(rowagree(t, Q, a - twopow(k), L)\), then if \(rowagree(t, Q, a, L)\), then \(rowagree(t + twopow(k), Q, a, L)\).
  \begin{proof}
  Close the goal with \(\lexeme{r30.witt::rowagree-late-of-winwittagree-and-three}(t, k, Q, a, L)\).

  \allowaxioms{Classical.choice;Quot.sound;propext}
  For every natural number \(k\) and natural number \(d\) and natural number \(U\) and natural number \(Q\), if \(2 ≤ U\), then if \(sectorwinwittagree(k, d, U, Q)\), then for every natural number \(t\), if \(d + U * twopow(k) ≤ t\), then \(wittresp(t + Q, k) = wittresp(t, k)\).

  \begin{theorem}{right-boundary-row-lcmperiod-eq}
  \allowaxioms{Classical.choice;Quot.sound;propext}
  For every natural number \(P\) and natural number \(k\) and natural number \(j\) and natural number \(t\), if \(j ≤ k\), then \(wittbitbool(wittrow(t + lcmperiod(P, k)), j) = wittbitbool(wittrow(t), j)\).

  \begin{theorem}{left-boundary-row-lcmperiod-eq}
  \allowaxioms{Classical.choice;Quot.sound;propext}
  For every natural number \(P\) and natural number \(k\) and natural number \(j\) and natural number \(t\), if \(j ≤ k\), then if \(j ≤ t\), then if \(settledby(j, t)\), then \(wittbitbool(wittrow(t + lcmperiod(P, k)), 2 * (t + lcmperiod(P, k)) - j) = wittbitbool(wittrow(t), 2 * t - j)\).

  Close the goal with \(\lexeme{r30.witt::left-boundary-row-lcmperiod-eq}(P, k, j, t)\).

  \begin{theorem}{frobdefect-row-sector-one-lcmperiod-xor}
  \allowaxioms{Classical.choice;Quot.sound;propext}
  For every natural number \(P\) and natural number \(d\) and natural number \(k\) and natural number \(u\), if \(d + 1 ≤ k\), then \(bxor(wittbitbool(frobdefect(k, wittrow(((d + 1) + u * twopow(k)) + lcmperiod(P, k))), (d + 1) + twopow(k)), wittbitbool(frobdefect(k, wittrow((d + 1) + u * twopow(k))), (d + 1) + twopow(k))) = bxor(bxor(rdiag((d + 1) + twopow(k), ((d + 1) + u * twopow(k)) + lcmperiod(P, k)), rdiag((d + 1) + twopow(k), (d + 1) + u * twopow(k))) band rdiag(d, d + 1), center(d + 1) band bxor(rdiag(d + twopow(k), ((d + 1) + u * twopow(k)) + lcmperiod(P, k)), rdiag(d + twopow(k), (d + 1) + u * twopow(k))))\).
  \begin{proof}
  Close the goal with \(\lexeme{r30.witt::frobdefect-row-sector-one-lcmperiod-xor}(P, d, k, u)\).

  \end{theorem}

  \begin{theorem}{rhcenter-eq-rdiag}
  \allowaxioms{Classical.choice;Quot.sound;propext}
  For every natural number \(t\) and natural number \(p\), if \(p ≤ t\), then \(rhcenter(t, p) = rdiag(t - p, t)\).

  Close the goal with \(\lexeme{r30.witt::rhcenter-eq-rdiag}(t, p)\).
  \end{proof}
  \end{theorem}

  \begin{theorem}{rhcenter-eq-row-testbit}
  \allowaxioms{Classical.choice;Quot.sound;propext}

  \begin{theorem}{lhcenter-eq-ldiag}
  \allowaxioms{Classical.choice;Quot.sound;propext}

  \begin{theorem}{lhcenter-eq-row-testbit}
  \allowaxioms{Classical.choice;Quot.sound;propext}
  For every natural number \(t\) and natural number \(p\), if \(p ≤ t\), then \(lhcenter(t, p) = wittbitbool(wittrow(t), t + p)\).

  \begin{theorem}{rhcenter-gt-eq-false}
  \allowaxioms{Classical.choice;Quot.sound;propext}

  For every natural number \(T\) and natural number \(P\), if \(0 < P\), then if \(centerperiodic(T, P)\), then for every natural number \(m\) and natural number \(t\), if \(T ≤ t\), then \(bxor(lhcenter(t + m * P, 1), lhcenter(t, 1)) = not(center(t)) band bxor(rhcenter(t + m * P, 1), rhcenter(t, 1))\).

  \begin{theorem}{rhcenter-step-succ}
  \allowaxioms{Classical.choice;Quot.sound;propext}

  \end{proof}
  \end{theorem}

  \allowaxioms{Classical.choice;Quot.sound;propext}

  \end{proof}
  \end{theorem}

  \begin{theorem}{periodic-rhoragree-one-of-col-one}
  \allowaxioms{Classical.choice;Quot.sound;propext}
  For every natural number \(T\) and natural number \(P\), if \(0 < P\), then if \(centerperiodic(T, P)\), then for every natural number \(m\) and natural number \(t\) and natural number \(S\), if \(T ≤ t\), then if for every natural number \(s\), if \(s < S\), then \(rhcenter((t + (s + 1)) + m * P, 1) = rhcenter(t + (s + 1), 1)\), then \(rhoragree(t, m * P, 1, S)\).
  \begin{proof}
  Assume \(hP\), \(hper\).
  Close the goal with \(\lexeme{r30.witt::periodic-rhoragree-one-of-col-one}(T, P, hP, hper)\).
  \end{proof}
  \end{theorem}

  \begin{theorem}{periodic-col-one-of-rhoragree-one}
  \allowaxioms{Classical.choice;Quot.sound;propext}
  For every natural number \(T\) and natural number \(P\), if \(0 < P\), then if \(centerperiodic(T, P)\), then for every natural number \(m\) and natural number \(t\) and natural number \(S\), if \(T ≤ t\), then if \(rhoragree(t, m * P, 1, S)\), then for every natural number \(s\), if \(s < S\), then \(rhcenter((t + (s + 1)) + m * P, 1) = rhcenter(t + (s + 1), 1)\).
  \begin{proof}
  Assume \(hP\), \(hper\).
  Close the goal with \(\lexeme{r30.witt::periodic-col-one-of-rhoragree-one}(T, P, hP, hper)\).
  \end{proof}
  \end{theorem}

  \begin{theorem}{rhcenter-inward-determine-step}
  \allowaxioms{Classical.choice;Quot.sound;propext}
  For every natural number \(t\) and natural number \(Q\) and natural number \(p0\) and natural number \(S\), if \(rhcolagree(t, Q, p0 + 1, S + 1)\), then if \(rhoragree(t, Q, p0 + 1, S + 1)\), then \(rhcolagree(t, Q, p0, S)\) and \(rhoragree(t, Q, p0, S)\).
  \begin{proof}
  Close the goal with \(\lexeme{r30.witt::rhcenter-inward-determine-step}(t, Q, p0, S)\).
  \end{proof}
  \end{theorem}

  \begin{theorem}{rhcenter-inward-determine}
  \allowaxioms{Classical.choice;Quot.sound;propext}
  For every natural number \(t\) and natural number \(Q\) and natural number \(p0\) and natural number \(S\) and natural number \(K\), if \(rhcolagree(t, Q, p0 + K, S + K)\), then if \(rhoragree(t, Q, p0 + K, S + K)\), then \(rhcolagree(t, Q, p0, S)\) and \(rhoragree(t, Q, p0, S)\).
  \begin{proof}
  Close the goal with \(\lexeme{r30.witt::rhcenter-inward-determine}(t, Q, p0, S, K)\).
  \end{proof}
  \end{theorem}

  \begin{theorem}{rhbandagree-of-inward-determine}
  \allowaxioms{Classical.choice;Quot.sound;propext}
  For every natural number \(t\) and natural number \(Q\) and natural number \(p0\) and natural number \(L\), if \(rhcolagree(t, Q, p0 + L, L)\), then if \(rhoragree(t, Q, p0 + L, L)\), then \(rhbandagree(t, Q, p0, L)\).
  \begin{proof}
  Close the goal with \(\lexeme{r30.witt::rhbandagree-of-inward-determine}(t, Q, p0, L)\).
  \end{proof}
  \end{theorem}

  \begin{theorem}{rhbandagree-outside-cone}
  \allowaxioms{Classical.choice;Quot.sound;propext}
  For every natural number \(t\) and natural number \(Q\) and natural number \(p0\) and natural number \(L\), if \(t + Q < p0\), then \(rhbandagree(t, Q, p0, L)\).
  \begin{proof}
  Close the goal with \(\lexeme{r30.witt::rhbandagree-outside-cone}(t, Q, p0, L)\).
  \end{proof}
  \end{theorem}

  \begin{theorem}{rhbandagree-scale-step}
  \allowaxioms{Classical.choice;Quot.sound;propext}
  For every natural number \(t\) and natural number \(k\) and natural number \(Q\) and natural number \(p0\) and natural number \(L\), if \((p0 + L) + twopow(succ(k)) ≤ t\), then if \(rhbandagree(t, Q, p0, L)\), then if \(rhbandagree(t, Q, p0 + twopow(k), L)\), then if \(rhbandagree(t, Q, p0 + twopow(succ(k)), L)\), then if \(winwittagree(t, k, Q, t - (p0 + L), L)\), then \(rhbandagree(t + twopow(k), Q, p0 + twopow(k), L)\).
  \begin{proof}
  Close the goal with \(\lexeme{r30.witt::rhbandagree-scale-step}(t, k, Q, p0, L)\).
  \end{proof}
  \end{theorem}

  \begin{theorem}{rhbandagree-scale-inward-step}
  \allowaxioms{Classical.choice;Quot.sound;propext}
  For every natural number \(t\) and natural number \(k\) and natural number \(Q\) and natural number \(p0\) and natural number \(L\), if \((p0 + L) + twopow(succ(k)) ≤ t\), then if \(rhbandagree(t + twopow(k), Q, p0 + twopow(k), L)\), then if \(rhbandagree(t, Q, p0 + twopow(k), L)\), then if \(rhbandagree(t, Q, p0 + twopow(succ(k)), L)\), then if \(winwittagree(t, k, Q, t - (p0 + L), L)\), then \(rhbandagree(t, Q, p0, L)\).
  \begin{proof}
  Close the goal with \(\lexeme{r30.witt::rhbandagree-scale-inward-step}(t, k, Q, p0, L)\).
  \end{proof}
  \end{theorem}

  \begin{theorem}{winwittagree-of-rhbandagree-scale}
  \allowaxioms{Classical.choice;Quot.sound;propext}
  For every natural number \(t\) and natural number \(k\) and natural number \(Q\) and natural number \(p0\) and natural number \(L\), if \((p0 + L) + twopow(succ(k)) ≤ t\), then if \(rhbandagree(t, Q, p0, L)\), then if \(rhbandagree(t, Q, p0 + twopow(k), L)\), then if \(rhbandagree(t, Q, p0 + twopow(succ(k)), L)\), then if \(rhbandagree(t + twopow(k), Q, p0 + twopow(k), L)\), then \(winwittagree(t, k, Q, t - (p0 + L), L)\).
  \begin{proof}
  Close the goal with \(\lexeme{r30.witt::winwittagree-of-rhbandagree-scale}(t, k, Q, p0, L)\).
  \end{proof}
  \end{theorem}

  \begin{theorem}{sectorrhbandagree-of-inward-determine}
  \allowaxioms{Classical.choice;Quot.sound;propext}
  For every natural number \(k\) and natural number \(d\) and natural number \(U\) and natural number \(Q\), if for every natural number \(u\), if \(U ≤ u\), then \(rhcolagree((d + u * twopow(k)) + 1, Q, twopow(k) + 1, twopow(k) + 1)\), then if for every natural number \(u\), if \(U ≤ u\), then \(rhoragree((d + u * twopow(k)) + 1, Q, twopow(k) + 1, twopow(k) + 1)\), then \(sectorrhbandagree(k, d, U, Q)\).
  \begin{proof}
  Close the goal with \(\lexeme{r30.witt::sectorrhbandagree-of-inward-determine}(k, d, U, Q)\).
  \end{proof}
  \end{theorem}

  \begin{theorem}{not-sector-inward-determine-lcmperiod}
  \allowaxioms{Classical.choice;Quot.sound;propext}
  For every natural number \(T\) and natural number \(P\), if \(0 < P\), then if \(centerperiodic(T, P)\), then for every natural number \(k\) and natural number \(d\) and natural number \(U\), if \(1 ≤ U\), then if \(1 ≤ d\), then if \(T ≤ d + U * twopow(k)\), then if for every natural number \(u\), if \(U ≤ u\), then \(rhcolagree((d + u * twopow(k)) + 1, lcmperiod(P, k), twopow(k) + 1, twopow(k) + 1)\), then not for every natural number \(u\), if \(U ≤ u\), then \(rhoragree((d + u * twopow(k)) + 1, lcmperiod(P, k), twopow(k) + 1, twopow(k) + 1)\).
  \begin{proof}
  Assume \(hP\), \(hper\), \(k\), \(d\), \(U\), \(hU\), \(hd\), \(ht\), \(hcol\).
  Close the goal with \(\lexeme{r30.witt::not-sector-inward-determine-lcmperiod}(T, P, hP, hper, k, d, U, hU, hd, ht, hcol)\).
  \end{proof}
  \end{theorem}

  \begin{theorem}{rhcenter-scale-step-le}
  \allowaxioms{Classical.choice;Quot.sound;propext}
  For every natural number \(t\) and natural number \(k\) and natural number \(p\), if \(p ≤ t\), then \(rhcenter(t + twopow(k), p + twopow(k)) = bxor(bxor(bxor(rhcenter(t, p), rhcenter(t, p + twopow(k))), rhcenter(t, p + twopow(succ(k)))), wittbitbool(wittwin(t, twopow(k)), t - p))\).
  \begin{proof}
  Close the goal with \(\lexeme{r30.witt::rhcenter-scale-step-le}(t, k, p)\).
  \end{proof}
  \end{theorem}

  \begin{theorem}{rhcenter-scale-inward-step-le}
  \allowaxioms{Classical.choice;Quot.sound;propext}
  For every natural number \(t\) and natural number \(k\) and natural number \(p\), if \(p ≤ t\), then \(rhcenter(t, p) = bxor(bxor(bxor(rhcenter(t + twopow(k), p + twopow(k)), rhcenter(t, p + twopow(k))), rhcenter(t, p + twopow(succ(k)))), wittbitbool(wittwin(t, twopow(k)), t - p))\).
  \begin{proof}
  Close the goal with \(\lexeme{r30.witt::rhcenter-scale-inward-step-le}(t, k, p)\).
  \end{proof}
  \end{theorem}

  \begin{theorem}{rhbandagree-scale-step-le}
  \allowaxioms{Classical.choice;Quot.sound;propext}
  For every natural number \(t\) and natural number \(k\) and natural number \(Q\) and natural number \(p0\) and natural number \(L\), if \(p0 + L ≤ t\), then if \(rhbandagree(t, Q, p0, L)\), then if \(rhbandagree(t, Q, p0 + twopow(k), L)\), then if \(rhbandagree(t, Q, p0 + twopow(succ(k)), L)\), then if \(winwittagree(t, k, Q, t - (p0 + L), L)\), then \(rhbandagree(t + twopow(k), Q, p0 + twopow(k), L)\).
  \begin{proof}

  \end{proof}
  \end{theorem}

  \begin{theorem}{rhbandagree-scale-inward-step-le}
  \allowaxioms{Classical.choice;Quot.sound;propext}
  For every natural number \(t\) and natural number \(k\) and natural number \(Q\) and natural number \(p0\) and natural number \(L\), if \(p0 + L ≤ t\), then if \(rhbandagree(t + twopow(k), Q, p0 + twopow(k), L)\), then if \(rhbandagree(t, Q, p0 + twopow(k), L)\), then if \(rhbandagree(t, Q, p0 + twopow(succ(k)), L)\), then if \(winwittagree(t, k, Q, t - (p0 + L), L)\), then \(rhbandagree(t, Q, p0, L)\).
  \begin{proof}
  Close the goal with \(\lexeme{r30.witt::rhbandagree-scale-inward-step-le}(t, k, Q, p0, L)\).

  \allowaxioms{Classical.choice;Quot.sound;propext}
  For every natural number \(t\) and natural number \(k\) and natural number \(Q\) and natural number \(p0\) and natural number \(L\), if \(p0 + L ≤ t\), then if \(rhbandagree(t, Q, p0, L)\), then if \(rhbandagree(t, Q, p0 + twopow(k), L)\), then if \(rhbandagree(t, Q, p0 + twopow(succ(k)), L)\), then if \(rhbandagree(t + twopow(k), Q, p0 + twopow(k), L)\), then \(winwittagree(t, k, Q, t - (p0 + L), L)\).
  \begin{proof}
  Close the goal with \(\lexeme{r30.witt::winwittagree-of-rhbandagree-scale-le}(t, k, Q, p0, L)\).
  \end{proof}
  \end{theorem}

  \begin{theorem}{rhbandagree-scale-inward-steps}
  \allowaxioms{Classical.choice;Quot.sound;propext}
  For every natural number \(t\) and natural number \(k\) and natural number \(Q\) and natural number \(p0\) and natural number \(L\) and natural number \(M\), if \((p0 + M * twopow(k)) + L ≤ t\), then if \(rhbandagree(t, Q, p0 + M * twopow(k), L)\), then if \(rhbandagree(t, Q, p0 + (M + 1) * twopow(k), L)\), then if for every natural number \(m\), if \(m < M\), then \(rhbandagree(t + twopow(k), Q, p0 + (m + 1) * twopow(k), L)\), then if for every natural number \(m\), if \(m < M\), then \(winwittagree(t, k, Q, t - ((p0 + m * twopow(k)) + L), L)\), then \(rhbandagree(t, Q, p0, L)\).
  \begin{proof}
  Close the goal with \(\lexeme{r30.witt::rhbandagree-scale-inward-steps}(t, k, Q, p0, L, M)\).
  \end{proof}
  \end{theorem}

  \begin{theorem}{rhcenter-inward-of-black-left}
  \allowaxioms{Classical.choice;Quot.sound;propext}
  For every natural number \(t\) and natural number \(p\), if \(rhcenter(t, p + 1) = true\), then \(rhcenter(t, p) = not(rhcenter(t + 1, p + 1))\).
  \begin{proof}
  Close the goal with \(\lexeme{r30.witt::rhcenter-inward-of-black-left}(t, p)\).
  \end{proof}
  \end{theorem}

  \begin{theorem}{rhcenter-inward-of-black-right}
  \allowaxioms{Classical.choice;Quot.sound;propext}
  For every natural number \(t\) and natural number \(p\), if \(rhcenter(t, p + 2) = true\), then \(rhcenter(t, p) = not(rhcenter(t + 1, p + 1))\).
  \begin{proof}
  Close the goal with \(\lexeme{r30.witt::rhcenter-inward-of-black-right}(t, p)\).
  \end{proof}
  \end{theorem}

  \begin{theorem}{rhcenter-agree-of-black-left}
  \allowaxioms{Classical.choice;Quot.sound;propext}
  For every natural number \(t\) and natural number \(Q\) and natural number \(p\), if \(rhcenter(t + Q, p + 1) = true\), then if \(rhcenter(t, p + 1) = true\), then if \(rhcenter((t + 1) + Q, p + 1) = rhcenter(t + 1, p + 1)\), then \(rhcenter(t + Q, p) = rhcenter(t, p)\).
  \begin{proof}
  Close the goal with \(\lexeme{r30.witt::rhcenter-agree-of-black-left}(t, Q, p)\).
  \end{proof}
  \end{theorem}

  \begin{theorem}{rhcenter-white-pair-of-diag-eq}
  \allowaxioms{Classical.choice;Quot.sound;propext}
  For every natural number \(t\) and natural number \(p\), if \(rhcenter(t + 1, p + 1) = rhcenter(t, p)\), then \(rhcenter(t, p + 1) = false\) and \(rhcenter(t, p + 2) = false\).
  \begin{proof}
  Close the goal with \(\lexeme{r30.witt::rhcenter-white-pair-of-diag-eq}(t, p)\).
  \end{proof}
  \end{theorem}

  \begin{theorem}{rhcenter-diag-eq-of-white-pair}
  \allowaxioms{Classical.choice;Quot.sound;propext}
  For every natural number \(t\) and natural number \(p\), if \(rhcenter(t, p + 1) = false\), then if \(rhcenter(t, p + 2) = false\), then \(rhcenter(t + 1, p + 1) = rhcenter(t, p)\).
  \begin{proof}
  Close the goal with \(\lexeme{r30.witt::rhcenter-diag-eq-of-white-pair}(t, p)\).
  \end{proof}
  \end{theorem}

  \begin{theorem}{rhcenter-diag-shift-twopow-eq-of-threshold}
  \allowaxioms{Classical.choice;Quot.sound;propext}
  For every natural number \(k\) and natural number \(J\) and natural number \(p\) and natural number \(s\) and natural number \(u\), if \(rightthreshold(k, J)\), then if \(s ≤ J\), then \(rhcenter((p + s) + u * twopow(k), p + u * twopow(k)) = rhcenter(p + s, p)\).
  \begin{proof}
  Close the goal with \(\lexeme{r30.witt::rhcenter-diag-shift-twopow-eq-of-threshold}(k, J, p, s, u)\).
  \end{proof}
  \end{theorem}

  \begin{theorem}{rhcenter-sector-diag-eq-center-of-threshold}
  \allowaxioms{Classical.choice;Quot.sound;propext}
  For every natural number \(k\) and natural number \(J\) and natural number \(s\) and natural number \(u\), if \(rightthreshold(k, J)\), then if \(s ≤ J\), then \(rhcenter(s + u * twopow(k), u * twopow(k)) = center(s)\).
  \begin{proof}
  Close the goal with \(\lexeme{r30.witt::rhcenter-sector-diag-eq-center-of-threshold}(k, J, s, u)\).
  \end{proof}
  \end{theorem}

  \begin{theorem}{rhcenter-zero-gap-of-threshold}
  \allowaxioms{Classical.choice;Quot.sound;propext}
  For every natural number \(k\) and natural number \(J\) and natural number \(u\) and natural number \(j\), if \(rightthreshold(k, J)\), then if \(1 ≤ j\), then if \(j ≤ J\), then if \(j ≤ u * twopow(k)\), then \(rhcenter(u * twopow(k), u * twopow(k) - j) = false\).
  \begin{proof}
  Close the goal with \(\lexeme{r30.witt::rhcenter-zero-gap-of-threshold}(k, J, u, j)\).
  \end{proof}
  \end{theorem}

  \begin{theorem}{rhcenter-last-one-of-threshold}
  \allowaxioms{Classical.choice;Quot.sound;propext}
  For every natural number \(k\) and natural number \(J\) and natural number \(u\) and natural number \(j\), if \(rightthreshold(k, J)\), then if \(j ≤ J\), then if \(j ≤ (u + 1) * twopow(k) - 1\), then \(rhcenter((u + 1) * twopow(k) - 1, ((u + 1) * twopow(k) - 1) - j) = true\).
  \begin{proof}
  Close the goal with \(\lexeme{r30.witt::rhcenter-last-one-of-threshold}(k, J, u, j)\).
  \end{proof}
  \end{theorem}

  \begin{theorem}{rhcenter-scale-step-uncond}
  \allowaxioms{Classical.choice;Quot.sound;propext}
  For every natural number \(t\) and natural number \(k\) and natural number \(p\), \(rhcenter(t + twopow(k), p + twopow(k)) = bxor(bxor(bxor(rhcenter(t, p), rhcenter(t, p + twopow(k))), rhcenter(t, p + twopow(succ(k)))), wittbitbool(wittwin(t, twopow(k)), t - p))\).
  \begin{proof}
  Close the goal with \(\lexeme{r30.witt::rhcenter-scale-step-uncond}(t, k, p)\).
  \end{proof}
  \end{theorem}

  \begin{theorem}{rhcenter-scale-inward-step-uncond}
  \allowaxioms{Classical.choice;Quot.sound;propext}
  For every natural number \(t\) and natural number \(k\) and natural number \(p\), \(rhcenter(t, p) = bxor(bxor(bxor(rhcenter(t + twopow(k), p + twopow(k)), rhcenter(t, p + twopow(k))), rhcenter(t, p + twopow(succ(k)))), wittbitbool(wittwin(t, twopow(k)), t - p))\).
  \begin{proof}
  Close the goal with \(\lexeme{r30.witt::rhcenter-scale-inward-step-uncond}(t, k, p)\).
  \end{proof}
  \end{theorem}

  \begin{theorem}{rhwinwittagree-of-winwittagree}
  \allowaxioms{Classical.choice;Quot.sound;propext}
  For every natural number \(t\) and natural number \(k\) and natural number \(Q\) and natural number \(p0\) and natural number \(L\), if \(p0 + L ≤ t\), then if \(winwittagree(t, k, Q, t - (p0 + L), L)\), then \(rhwinwittagree(t, k, Q, p0, L)\).
  \begin{proof}
  Close the goal with \(\lexeme{r30.witt::rhwinwittagree-of-winwittagree}(t, k, Q, p0, L)\).
  \end{proof}
  \end{theorem}

  \begin{theorem}{rhwinwittagree-outside-cone}
  \allowaxioms{Classical.choice;Quot.sound;propext}
  For every natural number \(t\) and natural number \(k\) and natural number \(Q\) and natural number \(p0\) and natural number \(L\), if \(t + Q ≤ p0\), then \(rhwinwittagree(t, k, Q, p0, L)\).
  \begin{proof}
  Close the goal with \(\lexeme{r30.witt::rhwinwittagree-outside-cone}(t, k, Q, p0, L)\).
  \end{proof}
  \end{theorem}

  \begin{theorem}{rhbandagree-scale-inward-step-uncond}
  \allowaxioms{Classical.choice;Quot.sound;propext}
  For every natural number \(t\) and natural number \(k\) and natural number \(Q\) and natural number \(p0\) and natural number \(L\), if \(rhbandagree(t + twopow(k), Q, p0 + twopow(k), L)\), then if \(rhbandagree(t, Q, p0 + twopow(k), L)\), then if \(rhbandagree(t, Q, p0 + twopow(succ(k)), L)\), then if \(rhwinwittagree(t, k, Q, p0, L)\), then \(rhbandagree(t, Q, p0, L)\).
  \begin{proof}
  Close the goal with \(\lexeme{r30.witt::rhbandagree-scale-inward-step-uncond}(t, k, Q, p0, L)\).
  \end{proof}
  \end{theorem}

  \begin{theorem}{rhbandagree-scale-inward-steps-uncond}
  \allowaxioms{Classical.choice;Quot.sound;propext}
  For every natural number \(t\) and natural number \(k\) and natural number \(Q\) and natural number \(p0\) and natural number \(L\) and natural number \(M\), if \(rhbandagree(t, Q, p0 + M * twopow(k), L)\), then if \(rhbandagree(t, Q, p0 + (M + 1) * twopow(k), L)\), then if for every natural number \(m\), if \(m < M\), then \(rhbandagree(t + twopow(k), Q, p0 + (m + 1) * twopow(k), L)\), then if for every natural number \(m\), if \(m < M\), then \(rhwinwittagree(t, k, Q, p0 + m * twopow(k), L)\), then \(rhbandagree(t, Q, p0, L)\).
  \begin{proof}
  Close the goal with \(\lexeme{r30.witt::rhbandagree-scale-inward-steps-uncond}(t, k, Q, p0, L, M)\).
  \end{proof}
  \end{theorem}

  \begin{theorem}{rhbandagree-of-scale-inward-cone}
  \allowaxioms{Classical.choice;Quot.sound;propext}
  For every natural number \(t\) and natural number \(k\) and natural number \(Q\) and natural number \(p0\) and natural number \(L\) and natural number \(M\), if \(t + Q < p0 + M * twopow(k)\), then if for every natural number \(m\), if \(m < M\), then \(rhbandagree(t + twopow(k), Q, p0 + (m + 1) * twopow(k), L)\), then if for every natural number \(m\), if \(m < M\), then \(rhwinwittagree(t, k, Q, p0 + m * twopow(k), L)\), then \(rhbandagree(t, Q, p0, L)\).
  \begin{proof}
  Close the goal with \(\lexeme{r30.witt::rhbandagree-of-scale-inward-cone}(t, k, Q, p0, L, M)\).
  \end{proof}
  \end{theorem}

  \begin{theorem}{rhcenter-diag-diff-eq-or}
  \allowaxioms{Classical.choice;Quot.sound;propext}
  For every natural number \(t\) and natural number \(p\), \(bxor(rhcenter(t + 1, p + 1), rhcenter(t, p)) = (rhcenter(t, p + 1) bor rhcenter(t, p + 2))\).
  \begin{proof}
  Close the goal with \(\lexeme{r30.witt::rhcenter-diag-diff-eq-or}(t, p)\).
  \end{proof}
  \end{theorem}

  \begin{theorem}{rhcenter-defect-diag-step}
  \allowaxioms{Classical.choice;Quot.sound;propext}
  For every natural number \(t\) and natural number \(Q\) and natural number \(p\), \(bxor(bxor(rhcenter((t + 1) + Q, p + 1), rhcenter(t + 1, p + 1)), bxor(rhcenter(t + Q, p), rhcenter(t, p))) = bxor(rhcenter(t + Q, p + 1) bor rhcenter(t + Q, p + 2), rhcenter(t, p + 1) bor rhcenter(t, p + 2))\).
  \begin{proof}
  Close the goal with \(\lexeme{r30.witt::rhcenter-defect-diag-step}(t, Q, p)\).
  \end{proof}
  \end{theorem}

  \begin{theorem}{rhcenter-defect-diag-step-of-col-eq}
  \allowaxioms{Classical.choice;Quot.sound;propext}
  For every natural number \(t\) and natural number \(Q\) and natural number \(p\), if \(rhcenter(t + Q, p + 1) = rhcenter(t, p + 1)\), then \(bxor(bxor(rhcenter((t + 1) + Q, p + 1), rhcenter(t + 1, p + 1)), bxor(rhcenter(t + Q, p), rhcenter(t, p))) = (not(rhcenter(t, p + 1)) band bxor(rhcenter(t + Q, p + 2), rhcenter(t, p + 2)))\).
  \begin{proof}
  Close the goal with \(\lexeme{r30.witt::rhcenter-defect-diag-step-of-col-eq}(t, Q, p)\).
  \end{proof}
  \end{theorem}

  \begin{theorem}{rhcenter-defect-inward-of-col-eq-two}
  \allowaxioms{Classical.choice;Quot.sound;propext}
  For every natural number \(t\) and natural number \(Q\) and natural number \(p\), if \(rhcenter(t + Q, p + 1) = rhcenter(t, p + 1)\), then if \(rhcenter((t + 1) + Q, p + 1) = rhcenter(t + 1, p + 1)\), then \(bxor(rhcenter(t + Q, p), rhcenter(t, p)) = (not(rhcenter(t, p + 1)) band bxor(rhcenter(t + Q, p + 2), rhcenter(t, p + 2)))\).
  \begin{proof}
  Close the goal with \(\lexeme{r30.witt::rhcenter-defect-inward-of-col-eq-two}(t, Q, p)\).
  \end{proof}
  \end{theorem}

  \begin{theorem}{rhcenter-outward-of-white-mid}
  \allowaxioms{Classical.choice;Quot.sound;propext}
  For every natural number \(t\) and natural number \(p\), if \(rhcenter(t, p + 1) = false\), then \(rhcenter(t, p + 2) = bxor(rhcenter(t + 1, p + 1), rhcenter(t, p))\).
  \begin{proof}
  Close the goal with \(\lexeme{r30.witt::rhcenter-outward-of-white-mid}(t, p)\).
  \end{proof}
  \end{theorem}

  \begin{theorem}{rhcenter-agree-outward-of-white-mid}
  \allowaxioms{Classical.choice;Quot.sound;propext}
  For every natural number \(t\) and natural number \(Q\) and natural number \(p\), if \(rhcenter(t, p + 1) = false\), then if \(rhcenter(t + Q, p) = rhcenter(t, p)\), then if \(rhcenter(t + Q, p + 1) = rhcenter(t, p + 1)\), then if \(rhcenter((t + 1) + Q, p + 1) = rhcenter(t + 1, p + 1)\), then \(rhcenter(t + Q, p + 2) = rhcenter(t, p + 2)\).
  \begin{proof}
  Close the goal with \(\lexeme{r30.witt::rhcenter-agree-outward-of-white-mid}(t, Q, p)\).
  \end{proof}
  \end{theorem}

  \begin{theorem}{rhcenter-defect-inward-of-white-mid}
  \allowaxioms{Classical.choice;Quot.sound;propext}
  For every natural number \(t\) and natural number \(Q\) and natural number \(p\), if \(rhcenter(t, p + 1) = false\), then if \(rhcenter(t + Q, p + 1) = rhcenter(t, p + 1)\), then if \(rhcenter((t + 1) + Q, p + 1) = rhcenter(t + 1, p + 1)\), then \(bxor(rhcenter(t + Q, p), rhcenter(t, p)) = bxor(rhcenter(t + Q, p + 2), rhcenter(t, p + 2))\).
  \begin{proof}
  Close the goal with \(\lexeme{r30.witt::rhcenter-defect-inward-of-white-mid}(t, Q, p)\).
  \end{proof}
  \end{theorem}

  \begin{theorem}{rhcenter-agree-inward-of-col-eq-two}
  \allowaxioms{Classical.choice;Quot.sound;propext}
  For every natural number \(t\) and natural number \(Q\) and natural number \(p\), if \(rhcenter(t + Q, p + 1) = rhcenter(t, p + 1)\), then if \(rhcenter((t + 1) + Q, p + 1) = rhcenter(t + 1, p + 1)\), then if \((not(rhcenter(t, p + 1)) band bxor(rhcenter(t + Q, p + 2), rhcenter(t, p + 2))) = false\), then \(rhcenter(t + Q, p) = rhcenter(t, p)\).
  \begin{proof}
  Close the goal with \(\lexeme{r30.witt::rhcenter-agree-inward-of-col-eq-two}(t, Q, p)\).
  \end{proof}
  \end{theorem}

  \begin{theorem}{not-lhbandagree-of-lt-add}
  \allowaxioms{Classical.choice;Quot.sound;propext}
  For every natural number \(t\) and natural number \(Q\) and natural number \(L\), if \(0 < Q\), then if \(t + Q ≤ L\), then not \(lhbandagree(t, Q, 0, L)\).
  \begin{proof}
  Close the goal with \(\lexeme{r30.witt::not-lhbandagree-of-lt-add}(t, Q, L)\).
  \end{proof}
  \end{theorem}

  \begin{theorem}{lhcenter-outward-determine-step}
  \allowaxioms{Classical.choice;Quot.sound;propext}
  For every natural number \(t\) and natural number \(Q\) and natural number \(p0\) and natural number \(S\), if \(lhcolagree(t, Q, p0, S + 1)\), then if \(lhcolagree(t, Q, p0 + 1, S + 1)\), then \(lhcolagree(t, Q, p0 + 2, S)\).
  \begin{proof}
  Close the goal with \(\lexeme{r30.witt::lhcenter-outward-determine-step}(t, Q, p0, S)\).
  \end{proof}
  \end{theorem}

  \begin{theorem}{lhcenter-outward-determine}
  \allowaxioms{Classical.choice;Quot.sound;propext}
  For every natural number \(t\) and natural number \(Q\) and natural number \(p0\) and natural number \(S\) and natural number \(K\), if \(lhcolagree(t, Q, p0, S + K)\), then if \(lhcolagree(t, Q, p0 + 1, S + K)\), then \(lhcolagree(t, Q, p0 + K, S)\) and \(lhcolagree(t, Q, (p0 + K) + 1, S)\).
  \begin{proof}
  Close the goal with \(\lexeme{r30.witt::lhcenter-outward-determine}(t, Q, p0, S, K)\).
  \end{proof}
  \end{theorem}

  \begin{theorem}{lhbandagree-of-outward-determine}
  \allowaxioms{Classical.choice;Quot.sound;propext}
  For every natural number \(t\) and natural number \(Q\) and natural number \(p0\) and natural number \(L\), if \(lhcolagree(t, Q, p0, L)\), then if \(lhcolagree(t, Q, p0 + 1, L)\), then \(lhbandagree(t, Q, p0, L + 1)\).
  \begin{proof}
  Close the goal with \(\lexeme{r30.witt::lhbandagree-of-outward-determine}(t, Q, p0, L)\).
  \end{proof}
  \end{theorem}

  \begin{theorem}{not-lhcolagree-zero-one-of-pos}
  \allowaxioms{Classical.choice;Quot.sound;propext}
  For every natural number \(t\) and natural number \(Q\) and natural number \(S\), if \(0 < Q\), then if \(t + Q ≤ S + 1\), then if \(lhcolagree(t, Q, 0, S)\), then not \(lhcolagree(t, Q, 1, S)\).

  \begin{theorem}{periodic-rhcolagree-one-of-finite-sectors}
  \allowaxioms{Classical.choice;Quot.sound;propext}
  For every natural number \(T\) and natural number \(P\), if \(0 < P\), then if \(centerperiodic(T, P)\), then for every natural number \(k\) and natural number \(d\) and natural number \(U\) and natural number \(M\) and natural number \(m\), if \(1 ≤ M\), then if \(T ≤ d + U * twopow(k)\), then if for every natural number \(j\), if \(j < M\), then \(rhbandagree((d + (U + j) * twopow(k)) + 1, m * P, 0, twopow(k) + 1)\), then \(rhcolagree((d + U * twopow(k)) + 1, m * P, 1, M * twopow(k))\).
  \begin{proof}
  Assume \(hP\), \(hper\).
  Close the goal with \(\lexeme{r30.witt::periodic-rhcolagree-one-of-finite-sectors}(T, P, hP, hper)\).

  \allowaxioms{Classical.choice;Quot.sound;propext}

  \allowaxioms{Classical.choice;Quot.sound;propext}
  For every natural number \(T\) and natural number \(P\), if \(0 < P\), then if \(centerperiodic(T, P)\), then for every natural number \(k\) and natural number \(d\) and natural number \(U\) and natural number \(M\), if \(1 ≤ M\), then if \(T ≤ d + U * twopow(k)\), then if \((d + U * twopow(k)) + lcmperiod(P, k) ≤ M * twopow(k)\), then not for every natural number \(j\), if \(j < M\), then \(rhbandagree((d + (U + j) * twopow(k)) + 1, lcmperiod(P, k), 0, twopow(k) + 1)\).

  \allowaxioms{Classical.choice;Quot.sound;propext}
  For every natural number \(T\) and natural number \(P\), if \(0 < P\), then if \(centerperiodic(T, P)\), then for every natural number \(m\) and natural number \(t\), if \(T ≤ t\), then \(bxor(lhcenter(t + m * P, 1) bor lhcenter(t + m * P, 0), lhcenter(t, 1) bor lhcenter(t, 0)) = bxor(lhcenter(t + m * P, 1), lhcenter(t, 1))\).
  \begin{proof}
  Assume \(hP\), \(hper\).
  Close the goal with \(\lexeme{r30.witt::periodic-lhcenter-or-zero-defect-eq}(T, P, hP, hper)\).

  \begin{theorem}{periodic-lhcenter-two-defect-eq}
  \allowaxioms{Classical.choice;Quot.sound;propext}
  For every natural number \(T\) and natural number \(P\), if \(0 < P\), then if \(centerperiodic(T, P)\), then for every natural number \(m\) and natural number \(t\), if \(T ≤ t\), then \(bxor(lhcenter(t + m * P, 2), lhcenter(t, 2)) = bxor(bxor(lhcenter((t + 1) + m * P, 1), lhcenter(t + 1, 1)), bxor(lhcenter(t + m * P, 1), lhcenter(t, 1)))\).
  \begin{proof}

  \end{theorem}

  \begin{theorem}{lhcenter-eq-row-testbit-uncond}
  \allowaxioms{Classical.choice;Quot.sound;propext}
  For every natural number \(t\) and natural number \(p\), \(lhcenter(t, p) = wittbitbool(wittrow(t), t + p)\).
  \begin{proof}
  Close the goal with \(\lexeme{r30.witt::lhcenter-eq-row-testbit-uncond}(t, p)\).
  \end{proof}
  \end{theorem}

  \begin{theorem}{lhcenter-scale-step-uncond}
  \allowaxioms{Classical.choice;Quot.sound;propext}
  For every natural number \(t\) and natural number \(k\) and natural number \(p\), \(lhcenter(t + twopow(k), p + twopow(k)) = bxor(bxor(bxor(lhcenter(t, p), lhcenter(t, p + twopow(k))), lhcenter(t, p + twopow(k + 1))), wittbitbool(wittwin(t, twopow(k)), (t + p) + twopow(k + 1)))\).
  \begin{proof}
  Close the goal with \(\lexeme{r30.witt::lhcenter-scale-step-uncond}(t, k, p)\).
  \end{proof}
  \end{theorem}

  \begin{theorem}{lhcenter-scale-outward-step-uncond}
  \allowaxioms{Classical.choice;Quot.sound;propext}
  For every natural number \(t\) and natural number \(k\) and natural number \(p\), \(lhcenter(t, p + twopow(k + 1)) = bxor(bxor(bxor(lhcenter(t + twopow(k), p + twopow(k)), lhcenter(t, p)), lhcenter(t, p + twopow(k))), wittbitbool(wittwin(t, twopow(k)), (t + p) + twopow(k + 1)))\).
  \begin{proof}
  Close the goal with \(\lexeme{r30.witt::lhcenter-scale-outward-step-uncond}(t, k, p)\).
  \end{proof}
  \end{theorem}

  \begin{theorem}{lhcenter-scale-cross-step}
  \allowaxioms{Classical.choice;Quot.sound;propext}
  For every natural number \(t\) and natural number \(k\) and natural number \(r\), if \(r ≤ twopow(k)\), then \(lhcenter(t + twopow(k), twopow(k) - r) = bxor(bxor(bxor(rhcenter(t, r), lhcenter(t, twopow(k) - r)), lhcenter(t, twopow(k + 1) - r)), wittbitbool(wittwin(t, twopow(k)), (t + twopow(k + 1)) - r))\).
  \begin{proof}
  Close the goal with \(\lexeme{r30.witt::lhcenter-scale-cross-step}(t, k, r)\).
  \end{proof}
  \end{theorem}

  \begin{theorem}{rhcenter-eq-lhcenter-scale-cross}
  \allowaxioms{Classical.choice;Quot.sound;propext}
  For every natural number \(t\) and natural number \(k\) and natural number \(r\), if \(r ≤ twopow(k)\), then \(rhcenter(t, r) = bxor(bxor(bxor(lhcenter(t + twopow(k), twopow(k) - r), lhcenter(t, twopow(k) - r)), lhcenter(t, twopow(k + 1) - r)), wittbitbool(wittwin(t, twopow(k)), (t + twopow(k + 1)) - r))\).
  \begin{proof}
  Close the goal with \(\lexeme{r30.witt::rhcenter-eq-lhcenter-scale-cross}(t, k, r)\).
  \end{proof}
  \end{theorem}

  \begin{theorem}{rhbandagree-of-lhbandagree-scale-cross}
  \allowaxioms{Classical.choice;Quot.sound;propext}
  For every natural number \(t\) and natural number \(k\) and natural number \(Q\) and natural number \(L\), if \(L ≤ twopow(k)\), then if \(lhbandagree(t + twopow(k), Q, twopow(k) - L, L)\), then if \(lhbandagree(t, Q, twopow(k) - L, L)\), then if \(lhbandagree(t, Q, twopow(k + 1) - L, L)\), then if \(winwittagree(t, k, Q, (t + twopow(k + 1)) - L, L)\), then \(rhbandagree(t, Q, 0, L)\).
  \begin{proof}
  Close the goal with \(\lexeme{r30.witt::rhbandagree-of-lhbandagree-scale-cross}(t, k, Q, L)\).
  \end{proof}
  \end{theorem}

  \begin{theorem}{winwittagree-of-rhbandagree-lhbandagree-scale-cross}
  \allowaxioms{Classical.choice;Quot.sound;propext}
  For every natural number \(t\) and natural number \(k\) and natural number \(Q\) and natural number \(L\), if \(L ≤ twopow(k)\), then if \(rhbandagree(t, Q, 0, L)\), then if \(lhbandagree(t + twopow(k), Q, twopow(k) - L, L)\), then if \(lhbandagree(t, Q, twopow(k) - L, L)\), then if \(lhbandagree(t, Q, twopow(k + 1) - L, L)\), then \(winwittagree(t, k, Q, (t + twopow(k + 1)) - L, L)\).
  \begin{proof}
  Close the goal with \(\lexeme{r30.witt::winwittagree-of-rhbandagree-lhbandagree-scale-cross}(t, k, Q, L)\).
  \end{proof}
  \end{theorem}

  \begin{theorem}{periodic-rhbandagree-of-lhcolagree-one-and-winwittagree}
  \allowaxioms{Classical.choice;Quot.sound;propext}
  For every natural number \(T\) and natural number \(P\), if \(0 < P\), then if \(centerperiodic(T, P)\), then for every natural number \(m\) and natural number \(t\) and natural number \(k\), if \(T ≤ t\), then if \(lhcolagree(t, m * P, 1, twopow(k + 1))\), then if \(winwittagree(t, k, m * P, t + twopow(k), twopow(k))\), then \(rhbandagree(t, m * P, 0, twopow(k))\).

  \allowaxioms{Classical.choice;Quot.sound;propext}
  For every natural number \(T\) and natural number \(P\), if \(0 < P\), then if \(centerperiodic(T, P)\), then for every natural number \(m\) and natural number \(t\) and natural number \(k\), if \(T ≤ t\), then if \(lhcolagree(t, m * P, 1, twopow(k + 1))\), then if \(rhbandagree(t, m * P, 0, twopow(k))\), then \(winwittagree(t, k, m * P, t + twopow(k), twopow(k))\).

  \begin{theorem}{lhbandagree-scale-step-uncond}
  \allowaxioms{Classical.choice;Quot.sound;propext}
  For every natural number \(t\) and natural number \(k\) and natural number \(Q\) and natural number \(p0\) and natural number \(L\), if \(lhbandagree(t, Q, p0, L)\), then if \(lhbandagree(t, Q, p0 + twopow(k), L)\), then if \(lhbandagree(t, Q, p0 + twopow(k + 1), L)\), then if \(winwittagree(t, k, Q, (t + p0) + twopow(k + 1), L)\), then \(lhbandagree(t + twopow(k), Q, p0 + twopow(k), L)\).

  \begin{theorem}{lhcenter-diag-diff-eq-or}
  \allowaxioms{Classical.choice;Quot.sound;propext}
  For every natural number \(t\) and natural number \(p\), \(bxor(lhcenter(t + 1, p + 1), lhcenter(t, p + 2)) = (lhcenter(t, p + 1) bor lhcenter(t, p))\).

  \begin{theorem}{lhcenter-defect-outward-step}
  \allowaxioms{Classical.choice;Quot.sound;propext}
  For every natural number \(t\) and natural number \(Q\) and natural number \(p\), \(bxor(lhcenter(t + Q, p + 2), lhcenter(t, p + 2)) = bxor(bxor(lhcenter((t + 1) + Q, p + 1), lhcenter(t + 1, p + 1)), bxor(lhcenter(t + Q, p + 1) bor lhcenter(t + Q, p), lhcenter(t, p + 1) bor lhcenter(t, p)))\).
  \begin{proof}
  Close the goal with \(\lexeme{r30.witt::lhcenter-defect-outward-step}(t, Q, p)\).

  \begin{theorem}{lhcenter-defect-outward-of-col-eq}
  \allowaxioms{Classical.choice;Quot.sound;propext}
  For every natural number \(t\) and natural number \(Q\) and natural number \(p\), if \(lhcenter(t + Q, p) = lhcenter(t, p)\), then \(bxor(lhcenter(t + Q, p + 2), lhcenter(t, p + 2)) = bxor(bxor(lhcenter((t + 1) + Q, p + 1), lhcenter(t + 1, p + 1)), not(lhcenter(t, p)) band bxor(lhcenter(t + Q, p + 1), lhcenter(t, p + 1)))\).
  \begin{proof}
  Close the goal with \(\lexeme{r30.witt::lhcenter-defect-outward-of-col-eq}(t, Q, p)\).
  \end{proof}
  \end{theorem}

  \begin{theorem}{lhcenter-defect-outward-of-black}
  \allowaxioms{Classical.choice;Quot.sound;propext}
  For every natural number \(t\) and natural number \(Q\) and natural number \(p\), if \(lhcenter(t + Q, p) = true\), then if \(lhcenter(t, p) = true\), then \(bxor(lhcenter(t + Q, p + 2), lhcenter(t, p + 2)) = bxor(lhcenter((t + 1) + Q, p + 1), lhcenter(t + 1, p + 1))\).
  \begin{proof}
  Close the goal with \(\lexeme{r30.witt::lhcenter-defect-outward-of-black}(t, Q, p)\).
  \end{proof}
  \end{theorem}

  \begin{theorem}{winwitt-testbit-left-edge}
  \allowaxioms{Classical.choice;Quot.sound;propext}
  For every natural number \(t\) and natural number \(k\), \(wittbitbool(wittwin(t, twopow(k)), (t + t) + twopow(k + 1)) = false\).
  \begin{proof}
  Close the goal with \(\lexeme{r30.witt::winwitt-testbit-left-edge}(t, k)\).
  \end{proof}
  \end{theorem}

  \begin{theorem}{winwitt-testbit-gt-left-edge}
  \allowaxioms{Classical.choice;Quot.sound;propext}
  For every natural number \(t\) and natural number \(k\) and natural number \(p\), if \(t < p\), then \(wittbitbool(wittwin(t, twopow(k)), (t + p) + twopow(k + 1)) = false\).
  \begin{proof}
  Close the goal with \(\lexeme{r30.witt::winwitt-testbit-gt-left-edge}(t, k, p)\).
  \end{proof}
  \end{theorem}

  \begin{theorem}{winwitt-testbit-ge-left-edge}
  \allowaxioms{Classical.choice;Quot.sound;propext}
  For every natural number \(t\) and natural number \(k\) and natural number \(p\), if \(t ≤ p\), then \(wittbitbool(wittwin(t, twopow(k)), (t + p) + twopow(k + 1)) = false\).
  \begin{proof}
  Close the goal with \(\lexeme{r30.witt::winwitt-testbit-ge-left-edge}(t, k, p)\).
  \end{proof}
  \end{theorem}

  \begin{theorem}{winwitt-testbit-settled-left}
  \allowaxioms{Classical.choice;Quot.sound;propext}
  For every natural number \(t\) and natural number \(k\) and natural number \(j\), if \(j ≤ k\), then if \(j ≤ t\), then if \(settledby(j, t)\), then \(wittbitbool(wittwin(t, twopow(k)), (t + (t - j)) + twopow(k + 1)) = false\).
  \begin{proof}
  Close the goal with \(\lexeme{r30.witt::winwitt-testbit-settled-left}(t, k, j)\).
  \end{proof}
  \end{theorem}

  \begin{theorem}{lhbandagree-outside-cone}
  \allowaxioms{Classical.choice;Quot.sound;propext}
  For every natural number \(t\) and natural number \(Q\) and natural number \(p0\) and natural number \(L\), if \(t + Q < p0\), then \(lhbandagree(t, Q, p0, L)\).
  \begin{proof}
  Close the goal with \(\lexeme{r30.witt::lhbandagree-outside-cone}(t, Q, p0, L)\).
  \end{proof}
  \end{theorem}

  \begin{theorem}{lhbandagree-mono}
  \allowaxioms{Classical.choice;Quot.sound;propext}
  For every natural number \(t\) and natural number \(Q\) and natural number \(p0\) and natural number \(L\) and natural number \(p0p\) and natural number \(Lp\), if \(p0 ≤ p0p\), then if \(p0p + Lp ≤ p0 + L\), then if \(lhbandagree(t, Q, p0, L)\), then \(lhbandagree(t, Q, p0p, Lp)\).
  \begin{proof}
  Close the goal with \(\lexeme{r30.witt::lhbandagree-mono}(t, Q, p0, L, p0p, Lp)\).
  \end{proof}
  \end{theorem}

  \begin{theorem}{lhcolagree-mono}
  \allowaxioms{Classical.choice;Quot.sound;propext}
  For every natural number \(t\) and natural number \(Q\) and natural number \(p0\) and natural number \(S\) and natural number \(s0\) and natural number \(Sp\), if \(s0 + Sp ≤ S\), then if \(lhcolagree(t, Q, p0, S)\), then \(lhcolagree(t + s0, Q, p0, Sp)\).
  \begin{proof}
  Close the goal with \(\lexeme{r30.witt::lhcolagree-mono}(t, Q, p0, S, s0, Sp)\).
  \end{proof}
  \end{theorem}

  \begin{theorem}{lhbandagree-step}
  \allowaxioms{Classical.choice;Quot.sound;propext}
  For every natural number \(t\) and natural number \(Q\) and natural number \(p0\) and natural number \(L\), if \(lhbandagree(t, Q, p0, L + 2)\), then \(lhbandagree(t + 1, Q, p0 + 1, L)\).
  \begin{proof}
  Close the goal with \(\lexeme{r30.witt::lhbandagree-step}(t, Q, p0, L)\).
  \end{proof}
  \end{theorem}

  \begin{theorem}{lhbandagree-steps}
  \allowaxioms{Classical.choice;Quot.sound;propext}
  For every natural number \(t\) and natural number \(Q\) and natural number \(p0\) and natural number \(L\) and natural number \(s\), if \(lhbandagree(t, Q, p0, L + 2 * s)\), then \(lhbandagree(t + s, Q, p0 + s, L)\).
  \begin{proof}
  Close the goal with \(\lexeme{r30.witt::lhbandagree-steps}(t, Q, p0, L, s)\).
  \end{proof}
  \end{theorem}

  \begin{theorem}{periodic-lhbandagree-zero-of-one}
  \allowaxioms{Classical.choice;Quot.sound;propext}
  For every natural number \(T\) and natural number \(P\), if \(0 < P\), then if \(centerperiodic(T, P)\), then for every natural number \(m\) and natural number \(t\) and natural number \(L\), if \(T ≤ t\), then if \(lhbandagree(t, m * P, 1, L)\), then \(lhbandagree(t, m * P, 0, L + 1)\).
  \begin{proof}
  Assume \(hP\), \(hper\).
  Close the goal with \(\lexeme{r30.witt::periodic-lhbandagree-zero-of-one}(T, P, hP, hper)\).
  \end{proof}
  \end{theorem}

  \begin{theorem}{periodic-rhbandagree-of-rhcolagree-one-and-winwittagree}
  \allowaxioms{Classical.choice;Quot.sound;propext}
  For every natural number \(T\) and natural number \(P\), if \(0 < P\), then if \(centerperiodic(T, P)\), then for every natural number \(m\) and natural number \(t\) and natural number \(k\), if \(T ≤ t\), then if \(rhcolagree(t, m * P, 1, twopow(k + 1))\), then if \(winwittagree(t, k, m * P, t + twopow(k), twopow(k))\), then \(rhbandagree(t, m * P, 0, twopow(k))\).
  \begin{proof}
  Assume \(hP\), \(hper\).
  Close the goal with \(\lexeme{r30.witt::periodic-rhbandagree-of-rhcolagree-one-and-winwittagree}(T, P, hP, hper)\).
  \end{proof}
  \end{theorem}

  \begin{theorem}{periodic-winwittagree-of-rhcolagree-one-and-rhbandagree}
  \allowaxioms{Classical.choice;Quot.sound;propext}
  For every natural number \(T\) and natural number \(P\), if \(0 < P\), then if \(centerperiodic(T, P)\), then for every natural number \(m\) and natural number \(t\) and natural number \(k\), if \(T ≤ t\), then if \(rhcolagree(t, m * P, 1, twopow(k + 1))\), then if \(rhbandagree(t, m * P, 0, twopow(k))\), then \(winwittagree(t, k, m * P, t + twopow(k), twopow(k))\).
  \begin{proof}
  Assume \(hP\), \(hper\).
  Close the goal with \(\lexeme{r30.witt::periodic-winwittagree-of-rhcolagree-one-and-rhbandagree}(T, P, hP, hper)\).
  \end{proof}
  \end{theorem}

  \begin{theorem}{lhcenter-scale-inward-step-uncond}
  \allowaxioms{Classical.choice;Quot.sound;propext}
  For every natural number \(t\) and natural number \(k\) and natural number \(p\), \(lhcenter(t, p) = bxor(bxor(bxor(lhcenter(t + twopow(k), p + twopow(k)), lhcenter(t, p + twopow(k))), lhcenter(t, p + twopow(k + 1))), wittbitbool(wittwin(t, twopow(k)), (t + p) + twopow(k + 1)))\).
  \begin{proof}
  Close the goal with \(\lexeme{r30.witt::lhcenter-scale-inward-step-uncond}(t, k, p)\).
  \end{proof}
  \end{theorem}

  \begin{theorem}{lhbandagree-scale-inward-step-uncond}
  \allowaxioms{Classical.choice;Quot.sound;propext}
  For every natural number \(t\) and natural number \(k\) and natural number \(Q\) and natural number \(p0\) and natural number \(L\), if \(lhbandagree(t + twopow(k), Q, p0 + twopow(k), L)\), then if \(lhbandagree(t, Q, p0 + twopow(k), L)\), then if \(lhbandagree(t, Q, p0 + twopow(k + 1), L)\), then if \(winwittagree(t, k, Q, (t + p0) + twopow(k + 1), L)\), then \(lhbandagree(t, Q, p0, L)\).
  \begin{proof}
  Close the goal with \(\lexeme{r30.witt::lhbandagree-scale-inward-step-uncond}(t, k, Q, p0, L)\).
  \end{proof}
  \end{theorem}

  \begin{theorem}{winwittagree-outside-left-cone}
  \allowaxioms{Classical.choice;Quot.sound;propext}
  For every natural number \(t\) and natural number \(k\) and natural number \(Q\) and natural number \(p0\) and natural number \(L\), if \(t + Q ≤ p0\), then \(winwittagree(t, k, Q, (t + p0) + twopow(k + 1), L)\).
  \begin{proof}
  Close the goal with \(\lexeme{r30.witt::winwittagree-outside-left-cone}(t, k, Q, p0, L)\).
  \end{proof}
  \end{theorem}

  \begin{theorem}{rdiag-add-eq-lhcenter-uncond}
  \allowaxioms{Classical.choice;Quot.sound;propext}
  For every natural number \(t\) and natural number \(p\), \(rdiag(t + p, t) = lhcenter(t, p)\).
  \begin{proof}
  Close the goal with \(\lexeme{r30.witt::rdiag-add-eq-lhcenter-uncond}(t, p)\).
  \end{proof}
  \end{theorem}

  \begin{theorem}{winwitt-testbit-eq-lhcenter-scale-uncond}
  \allowaxioms{Classical.choice;Quot.sound;propext}
  For every natural number \(t\) and natural number \(k\) and natural number \(p\), \(wittbitbool(wittwin(t, twopow(k)), (t + p) + twopow(k + 1)) = bxor(bxor(bxor(lhcenter(t + twopow(k), p + twopow(k)), lhcenter(t, p)), lhcenter(t, p + twopow(k))), lhcenter(t, p + twopow(k + 1)))\).
  \begin{proof}
  Close the goal with \(\lexeme{r30.witt::winwitt-testbit-eq-lhcenter-scale-uncond}(t, k, p)\).
  \end{proof}
  \end{theorem}

  \begin{theorem}{winwittagree-of-lhbandagree-scale-uncond}
  \allowaxioms{Classical.choice;Quot.sound;propext}
  For every natural number \(t\) and natural number \(k\) and natural number \(Q\) and natural number \(p0\) and natural number \(L\), if \(lhbandagree(t + twopow(k), Q, p0 + twopow(k), L)\), then if \(lhbandagree(t, Q, p0, L)\), then if \(lhbandagree(t, Q, p0 + twopow(k), L)\), then if \(lhbandagree(t, Q, p0 + twopow(k + 1), L)\), then \(winwittagree(t, k, Q, (t + p0) + twopow(k + 1), L)\).
  \begin{proof}
  Close the goal with \(\lexeme{r30.witt::winwittagree-of-lhbandagree-scale-uncond}(t, k, Q, p0, L)\).
  \end{proof}
  \end{theorem}

  \begin{theorem}{lhcenter-scale-mid-step-uncond}
  \allowaxioms{Classical.choice;Quot.sound;propext}
  For every natural number \(t\) and natural number \(k\) and natural number \(p\), \(lhcenter(t, p + twopow(k)) = bxor(bxor(bxor(lhcenter(t + twopow(k), p + twopow(k)), lhcenter(t, p)), lhcenter(t, p + twopow(k + 1))), wittbitbool(wittwin(t, twopow(k)), (t + p) + twopow(k + 1)))\).
  \begin{proof}
  Close the goal with \(\lexeme{r30.witt::lhcenter-scale-mid-step-uncond}(t, k, p)\).
  \end{proof}
  \end{theorem}

  \begin{theorem}{lhbandagree-scale-mid-step-uncond}
  \allowaxioms{Classical.choice;Quot.sound;propext}
  For every natural number \(t\) and natural number \(k\) and natural number \(Q\) and natural number \(p0\) and natural number \(L\), if \(lhbandagree(t + twopow(k), Q, p0 + twopow(k), L)\), then if \(lhbandagree(t, Q, p0, L)\), then if \(lhbandagree(t, Q, p0 + twopow(k + 1), L)\), then if \(winwittagree(t, k, Q, (t + p0) + twopow(k + 1), L)\), then \(lhbandagree(t, Q, p0 + twopow(k), L)\).
  \begin{proof}
  Close the goal with \(\lexeme{r30.witt::lhbandagree-scale-mid-step-uncond}(t, k, Q, p0, L)\).
  \end{proof}
  \end{theorem}

  \begin{theorem}{lhcenter-scale-step-outer-strip}
  \allowaxioms{Classical.choice;Quot.sound;propext}
  For every natural number \(t\) and natural number \(k\) and natural number \(p\), if \(t < p + twopow(k)\), then \(lhcenter(t + twopow(k), p + twopow(k)) = bxor(lhcenter(t, p), wittbitbool(wittwin(t, twopow(k)), (t + p) + twopow(k + 1)))\).
  \begin{proof}
  Close the goal with \(\lexeme{r30.witt::lhcenter-scale-step-outer-strip}(t, k, p)\).
  \end{proof}
  \end{theorem}

  \begin{theorem}{lhcenter-scale-inward-outer-strip}
  \allowaxioms{Classical.choice;Quot.sound;propext}
  For every natural number \(t\) and natural number \(k\) and natural number \(p\), if \(t < p + twopow(k)\), then \(lhcenter(t, p) = bxor(lhcenter(t + twopow(k), p + twopow(k)), wittbitbool(wittwin(t, twopow(k)), (t + p) + twopow(k + 1)))\).
  \begin{proof}
  Close the goal with \(\lexeme{r30.witt::lhcenter-scale-inward-outer-strip}(t, k, p)\).
  \end{proof}
  \end{theorem}

  \begin{theorem}{winwitt-testbit-outer-left-strip}
  \allowaxioms{Classical.choice;Quot.sound;propext}
  For every natural number \(t\) and natural number \(k\) and natural number \(p\), if \(t < p + twopow(k)\), then \(wittbitbool(wittwin(t, twopow(k)), (t + p) + twopow(k + 1)) = bxor(lhcenter(t + twopow(k), p + twopow(k)), lhcenter(t, p))\).
  \begin{proof}
  Close the goal with \(\lexeme{r30.witt::winwitt-testbit-outer-left-strip}(t, k, p)\).
  \end{proof}
  \end{theorem}

  \begin{theorem}{winwitt-testbit-outer-left-strip-eq-ldiag-xor}
  \allowaxioms{Classical.choice;Quot.sound;propext}
  For every natural number \(t\) and natural number \(k\) and natural number \(p\), if \(p ≤ t\), then if \(t < p + twopow(k)\), then \(wittbitbool(wittwin(t, twopow(k)), (t + p) + twopow(k + 1)) = bxor(ldiag(t - p, t + twopow(k)), ldiag(t - p, t))\).
  \begin{proof}
  Close the goal with \(\lexeme{r30.witt::winwitt-testbit-outer-left-strip-eq-ldiag-xor}(t, k, p)\).
  \end{proof}
  \end{theorem}

  \begin{theorem}{lhbandagree-scale-inward-outer-strip}
  \allowaxioms{Classical.choice;Quot.sound;propext}
  For every natural number \(t\) and natural number \(k\) and natural number \(Q\) and natural number \(p0\) and natural number \(L\), if \(t + Q < p0 + twopow(k)\), then if \(lhbandagree(t + twopow(k), Q, p0 + twopow(k), L)\), then if \(winwittagree(t, k, Q, (t + p0) + twopow(k + 1), L)\), then \(lhbandagree(t, Q, p0, L)\).
  \begin{proof}
  Close the goal with \(\lexeme{r30.witt::lhbandagree-scale-inward-outer-strip}(t, k, Q, p0, L)\).
  \end{proof}
  \end{theorem}

  \begin{theorem}{lhbandagree-scale-step-outer-strip}
  \allowaxioms{Classical.choice;Quot.sound;propext}
  For every natural number \(t\) and natural number \(k\) and natural number \(Q\) and natural number \(p0\) and natural number \(L\), if \(t + Q < p0 + twopow(k)\), then if \(lhbandagree(t, Q, p0, L)\), then if \(winwittagree(t, k, Q, (t + p0) + twopow(k + 1), L)\), then \(lhbandagree(t + twopow(k), Q, p0 + twopow(k), L)\).
  \begin{proof}
  Close the goal with \(\lexeme{r30.witt::lhbandagree-scale-step-outer-strip}(t, k, Q, p0, L)\).

  \begin{theorem}{winwittagree-outer-left-strip}
  \allowaxioms{Classical.choice;Quot.sound;propext}
  For every natural number \(t\) and natural number \(k\) and natural number \(Q\) and natural number \(p0\) and natural number \(L\), if \(t + Q < p0 + twopow(k)\), then if \(lhbandagree(t + twopow(k), Q, p0 + twopow(k), L)\), then if \(lhbandagree(t, Q, p0, L)\), then \(winwittagree(t, k, Q, (t + p0) + twopow(k + 1), L)\).
  \begin{proof}
  Close the goal with \(\lexeme{r30.witt::winwittagree-outer-left-strip}(t, k, Q, p0, L)\).
  \end{proof}
  \end{theorem}

  \begin{theorem}{not-lhbandagree-and-winwittagree-of-lt-twopow}
  \allowaxioms{Classical.choice;Quot.sound;propext}
  For every natural number \(t\) and natural number \(k\) and natural number \(Q\) and natural number \(L\), if \(0 < Q\), then if \(t + Q ≤ L\), then if \(t + Q < twopow(k)\), then if \(lhbandagree(t + twopow(k), Q, twopow(k), L)\), then not \(winwittagree(t, k, Q, t + twopow(k + 1), L)\).
  \begin{proof}
  Close the goal with \(\lexeme{r30.witt::not-lhbandagree-and-winwittagree-of-lt-twopow}(t, k, Q, L)\).
  \end{proof}
  \end{theorem}

  \begin{theorem}{frobdefect-row-testbit-lhcenter-uncond}
  \allowaxioms{Classical.choice;Quot.sound;propext}
  For every natural number \(t\) and natural number \(k\) and natural number \(p\), \(wittbitbool(frobdefect(k, wittrow(t)), ((t + p) + 1) + twopow(k + 1)) = bxor(bxor(lhcenter(t, (p + 1) + twopow(k + 1)) band bxor(lhcenter(t, p + twopow(k)), lhcenter(t, p)), lhcenter(t, (p + 1) + twopow(k)) band bxor(lhcenter(t, p + twopow(k + 1)), lhcenter(t, p))), lhcenter(t, p + 1) band bxor(lhcenter(t, p + twopow(k + 1)), lhcenter(t, p + twopow(k))))\).
  \begin{proof}
  Close the goal with \(\lexeme{r30.witt::frobdefect-row-testbit-lhcenter-uncond}(t, k, p)\).
  \end{proof}
  \end{theorem}

  \begin{theorem}{frobdefect-row-testbit-lhcenter-outer-strip-eq-false}
  \allowaxioms{Classical.choice;Quot.sound;propext}
  For every natural number \(t\) and natural number \(k\) and natural number \(p\), if \(t < p + twopow(k)\), then \(wittbitbool(frobdefect(k, wittrow(t)), ((t + p) + 1) + twopow(k + 1)) = false\).

  \begin{theorem}{phiscale-row-testbit-lhcenter-uncond}
  \allowaxioms{Classical.choice;Quot.sound;propext}
  For every natural number \(t\) and natural number \(k\) and natural number \(p\), \(wittbitbool(wittscale(k, wittrow(t)), (t + p) + twopow(k + 1)) = bxor(bxor(lhcenter(t, p + twopow(k + 1)), lhcenter(t, p + twopow(k))), lhcenter(t, p))\).

  \begin{theorem}{phiscale-row-testbit-lhcenter-outer-strip}
  \allowaxioms{Classical.choice;Quot.sound;propext}
  For every natural number \(t\) and natural number \(k\) and natural number \(p\), if \(t < p + twopow(k)\), then \(wittbitbool(wittscale(k, wittrow(t)), (t + p) + twopow(k + 1)) = lhcenter(t, p)\).

  Close the goal with \(\lexeme{r30.witt::phiscale-row-testbit-lhcenter-outer-strip}(t, k, p)\).
  \end{proof}
  \end{theorem}

  \begin{theorem}{crosscarry-row-scale-lhcenter-outer-strip}
  \allowaxioms{Classical.choice;Quot.sound;propext}
  For every natural number \(t\) and natural number \(k\) and natural number \(p\), if \(t < p + twopow(k)\), then \(wittbitbool(crosscarry(wittscale(k, wittrow(t)), wittwin(t, twopow(k))), ((t + p) + 1) + twopow(k + 1)) = bxor(lhcenter(t, p + 1) band wittbitbool(wittwin(t, twopow(k)), (t + p) + twopow(k + 1)), wittbitbool(wittwin(t, twopow(k)), (t + (p + 1)) + twopow(k + 1)) band lhcenter(t, p))\).
  \begin{proof}
  Close the goal with \(\lexeme{r30.witt::crosscarry-row-scale-lhcenter-outer-strip}(t, k, p)\).
  \end{proof}
  \end{theorem}

  \begin{theorem}{winwitt-step-outer-left-strip}
  \allowaxioms{Classical.choice;Quot.sound;propext}
  For every natural number \(t\) and natural number \(k\) and natural number \(p\), if \(t < p + twopow(k)\), then \(wittbitbool(wittwin(t + 1, twopow(k)), ((t + 1) + (p + 1)) + twopow(k + 1)) = bxor(wittbitbool(wittphi(wittwin(t, twopow(k))), ((t + 1) + (p + 1)) + twopow(k + 1)), bxor(lhcenter(t, p + 1) band wittbitbool(wittwin(t, twopow(k)), (t + p) + twopow(k + 1)), wittbitbool(wittwin(t, twopow(k)), (t + (p + 1)) + twopow(k + 1)) band lhcenter(t, p)))\).
  \begin{proof}
  Close the goal with \(\lexeme{r30.witt::winwitt-step-outer-left-strip}(t, k, p)\).
  \end{proof}
  \end{theorem}

  \begin{theorem}{winwittagree-step-outer-left-strip}
  \allowaxioms{Classical.choice;Quot.sound;propext}

  For every natural number \(t\) and natural number \(k\) and natural number \(Q\) and natural number \(p0\) and natural number \(L\), if \(((t + Q) + p0) + L < twopow(k)\), then if \(lhbandagree(t, Q, p0, L)\), then if \(winwittagree(t, k, Q, (t + p0) + twopow(k), L)\), then \(lhbandagree(t + twopow(k), Q, p0, L)\).
  \begin{proof}
  Close the goal with \(\lexeme{r30.witt::lhbandagree-scale-same-col-cone}(t, k, Q, p0, L)\).
  \end{proof}
  \end{theorem}

  \begin{theorem}{lhbandagree-scale-inward-same-col-cone}
  \allowaxioms{Classical.choice;Quot.sound;propext}
  For every natural number \(t\) and natural number \(k\) and natural number \(Q\) and natural number \(p0\) and natural number \(L\), if \(((t + Q) + p0) + L < twopow(k)\), then if \(lhbandagree(t + twopow(k), Q, p0, L)\), then if \(winwittagree(t, k, Q, (t + p0) + twopow(k), L)\), then \(lhbandagree(t, Q, p0, L)\).

  Close the goal with \(\lexeme{r30.witt::lhbandagree-scale-inward-same-col-cone}(t, k, Q, p0, L)\).
  \end{proof}
  \end{theorem}

  \begin{theorem}{winwittagree-sector-one-lhcenter}
  \allowaxioms{Classical.choice;Quot.sound;propext}
  For every natural number \(t\) and natural number \(k\) and natural number \(Q\) and natural number \(p0\) and natural number \(L\), if \(((t + Q) + p0) + L < twopow(k)\), then if \(lhbandagree(t + twopow(k), Q, p0, L)\), then if \(lhbandagree(t, Q, p0, L)\), then \(winwittagree(t, k, Q, (t + p0) + twopow(k), L)\).
  \begin{proof}
  Close the goal with \(\lexeme{r30.witt::winwittagree-sector-one-lhcenter}(t, k, Q, p0, L)\).

  \begin{theorem}{not-lhbandagree-late-and-winwittagree-sector-one}
  \allowaxioms{Classical.choice;Quot.sound;propext}
  For every natural number \(t\) and natural number \(k\) and natural number \(Q\) and natural number \(L\), if \(0 < Q\), then if \(t + Q ≤ L\), then if \((t + Q) + L < twopow(k)\), then if \(lhbandagree(t + twopow(k), Q, 0, L)\), then not \(winwittagree(t, k, Q, t + twopow(k), L)\).
  \begin{proof}
  Close the goal with \(\lexeme{r30.witt::not-lhbandagree-late-and-winwittagree-sector-one}(t, k, Q, L)\).

  \end{theorem}

  \begin{theorem}{frobdefect-row-testbit-lhcenter-sector-one-eq-false}
  \allowaxioms{Classical.choice;Quot.sound;propext}
  For every natural number \(t\) and natural number \(k\) and natural number \(p\), if \((t + p) + 1 < twopow(k)\), then \(wittbitbool(frobdefect(k, wittrow(t)), ((t + p) + 1) + twopow(k)) = false\).
  \begin{proof}
  Close the goal with \(\lexeme{r30.witt::frobdefect-row-testbit-lhcenter-sector-one-eq-false}(t, k, p)\).
  \end{proof}
  \end{theorem}

  \begin{theorem}{crosscarry-row-scale-lhcenter-sector-one}
  \allowaxioms{Classical.choice;Quot.sound;propext}
  For every natural number \(t\) and natural number \(k\) and natural number \(p\), if \((t + p) + 1 < twopow(k)\), then \(wittbitbool(crosscarry(wittscale(k, wittrow(t)), wittwin(t, twopow(k))), ((t + p) + 1) + twopow(k)) = bxor(lhcenter(t, p + 1) band wittbitbool(wittwin(t, twopow(k)), (t + p) + twopow(k)), wittbitbool(wittwin(t, twopow(k)), (t + (p + 1)) + twopow(k)) band lhcenter(t, p))\).
  \begin{proof}
  Close the goal with \(\lexeme{r30.witt::crosscarry-row-scale-lhcenter-sector-one}(t, k, p)\).
  \end{proof}
  \end{theorem}

  \begin{theorem}{winwitt-step-sector-one-lhcenter}
  \allowaxioms{Classical.choice;Quot.sound;propext}
  For every natural number \(t\) and natural number \(k\) and natural number \(p\), if \((t + p) + 1 < twopow(k)\), then \(wittbitbool(wittwin(t + 1, twopow(k)), ((t + 1) + (p + 1)) + twopow(k)) = bxor(wittbitbool(wittphi(wittwin(t, twopow(k))), ((t + 1) + (p + 1)) + twopow(k)), bxor(lhcenter(t, p + 1) band wittbitbool(wittwin(t, twopow(k)), (t + p) + twopow(k)), wittbitbool(wittwin(t, twopow(k)), (t + (p + 1)) + twopow(k)) band lhcenter(t, p)))\).

  \begin{theorem}{winwittagree-step-sector-one-lhcenter}
  \allowaxioms{Classical.choice;Quot.sound;propext}
  For every natural number \(t\) and natural number \(k\) and natural number \(Q\) and natural number \(p0\) and natural number \(L\), if \((((t + Q) + p0) + L) + 1 < twopow(k)\), then if \(lhbandagree(t, Q, p0, L + 1)\), then if \(winwittagree(t, k, Q, (t + p0) + twopow(k), L + 2)\), then \(winwittagree(t + 1, k, Q, ((t + 1) + (p0 + 1)) + twopow(k), L)\).
  \begin{proof}
  Close the goal with \(\lexeme{r30.witt::winwittagree-step-sector-one-lhcenter}(t, k, Q, p0, L)\).

  \begin{theorem}{winwittagree-outward-step-sector-one-lhcenter}
  \allowaxioms{Classical.choice;Quot.sound;propext}

  \begin{proof}
  Close the goal with \(\lexeme{r30.witt::winwittagree-outward-step-sector-one-lhcenter}(t, k, Q, p0, L)\).

  \begin{theorem}{periodic-winwitt-sector-one-zero-eq}
  \allowaxioms{Classical.choice;Quot.sound;propext}
  For every natural number \(T\) and natural number \(P\), if \(0 < P\), then if \(centerperiodic(T, P)\), then for every natural number \(m\) and natural number \(t\) and natural number \(k\), if \(T ≤ t\), then if \(t + m * P < twopow(k)\), then \(wittbitbool(wittwin(t + m * P, twopow(k)), (t + m * P) + twopow(k)) = wittbitbool(wittwin(t, twopow(k)), t + twopow(k))\).

  Assume \(hP\), \(hper\).
  Close the goal with \(\lexeme{r30.witt::periodic-winwitt-sector-one-zero-eq}(T, P, hP, hper)\).
  \end{proof}
  \end{theorem}

  \begin{theorem}{periodic-winwitt-sector-one-two-defect-eq}
  \allowaxioms{Classical.choice;Quot.sound;propext}
  For every natural number \(T\) and natural number \(P\), if \(0 < P\), then if \(centerperiodic(T, P)\), then for every natural number \(m\) and natural number \(t\) and natural number \(k\), if \(T ≤ t\), then if \((t + m * P) + 2 < twopow(k)\), then \(bxor(wittbitbool(wittwin(t + m * P, twopow(k)), ((t + m * P) + 2) + twopow(k)), wittbitbool(wittwin(t, twopow(k)), (t + 2) + twopow(k))) = bxor(bxor(wittbitbool(wittwin((t + 1) + m * P, twopow(k)), (((t + 1) + m * P) + 1) + twopow(k)), wittbitbool(wittwin(t + 1, twopow(k)), ((t + 1) + 1) + twopow(k))), bxor(wittbitbool(wittwin(t + m * P, twopow(k)), ((t + m * P) + 1) + twopow(k)), wittbitbool(wittwin(t, twopow(k)), (t + 1) + twopow(k))))\).
  \begin{proof}
  Assume \(hP\), \(hper\).
  Close the goal with \(\lexeme{r30.witt::periodic-winwitt-sector-one-two-defect-eq}(T, P, hP, hper)\).
  \end{proof}

  \allowaxioms{Classical.choice;Quot.sound;propext}

  \allowaxioms{Classical.choice;Quot.sound;propext}

  \allowaxioms{Classical.choice;Quot.sound;propext}
  For every natural number \(t\) and natural number \(Q\) and natural number \(R\), if \(R ≤ t\), then if \(rowagree(t, Q, t - R, 2 * R)\), then \(lhbandagree(t, Q, 0, R)\).

  \allowaxioms{Classical.choice;Quot.sound;propext}
  For every natural number \(T\) and natural number \(P\), if \(0 < P\), then if \(centerperiodic(T, P)\), then for every natural number \(m\) and natural number \(t\) and natural number \(k\) and natural number \(R\), if \(T ≤ t\), then if \(R ≤ t\), then if \((t + m * P) + R < twopow(k)\), then if \(rhbandagree(t, m * P, 0, R)\), then if \(winwittagree(t, k, m * P, (t + twopow(k)) - R, R)\), then \(winwittagree(t, k, m * P, t + twopow(k), R)\).

  \allowaxioms{Classical.choice;Quot.sound;propext}

  \begin{theorem}{winwittagree-step-rhcenter-cone}
  \allowaxioms{Classical.choice;Quot.sound;propext}
  For every natural number \(t\) and natural number \(k\) and natural number \(Q\) and natural number \(p0\) and natural number \(L\), if \((((t + Q) + p0) + L) + 1 < twopow(k)\), then if \(rhbandagree(t, Q, p0, L + 1)\), then if \(winwittagree(t, k, Q, (t + twopow(k)) - ((p0 + L) + 1), L + 2)\), then \(winwittagree(t + 1, k, Q, ((t + 1) + twopow(k)) - (p0 + L), L)\).

  \begin{theorem}{winwittagree-inward-step-rhcenter-cone}

  \begin{theorem}{winwitt-step-inter-cone-gap-eq-phi}
  \allowaxioms{Classical.choice;Quot.sound;propext}
  For every natural number \(t\) and natural number \(k\) and natural number \(b\), if \(2 * t + 1 < b\), then if \(b < twopow(k)\), then \(wittbitbool(wittwin(t + 1, twopow(k)), b + 1) = wittbitbool(wittphi(wittwin(t, twopow(k))), b + 1)\).
  \begin{proof}
  Close the goal with \(\lexeme{r30.witt::winwitt-step-inter-cone-gap-eq-phi}(t, k, b)\).
  \end{proof}
  \end{theorem}

  \begin{theorem}{winwitt-inward-step-inter-cone-gap}
  \allowaxioms{Classical.choice;Quot.sound;propext}
  For every natural number \(t\) and natural number \(k\) and natural number \(b\), if \(2 * t + 1 < b\), then if \(b < twopow(k)\), then \(wittbitbool(wittwin(t, twopow(k)), b + 1) = bxor(wittbitbool(wittwin(t + 1, twopow(k)), b + 1), wittbitbool(wittwin(t, twopow(k)), b) bor wittbitbool(wittwin(t, twopow(k)), b - 1))\).
  \begin{proof}
  Close the goal with \(\lexeme{r30.witt::winwitt-inward-step-inter-cone-gap}(t, k, b)\).
  \end{proof}
  \end{theorem}

  \begin{theorem}{phiscale-row-testbit-sector-one-eq-row}
  \allowaxioms{Classical.choice;Quot.sound;propext}
  For every natural number \(t\) and natural number \(k\) and natural number \(d\), if \(2 * t < twopow(k)\), then if \(d < twopow(k)\), then \(wittbitbool(wittscale(k, wittrow(t)), d + twopow(k)) = wittbitbool(wittrow(t), d)\).
  \begin{proof}
  Close the goal with \(\lexeme{r30.witt::phiscale-row-testbit-sector-one-eq-row}(t, k, d)\).
  \end{proof}
  \end{theorem}

  \begin{theorem}{phiscale-row-testbit-sector-two-eq-row}
  \allowaxioms{Classical.choice;Quot.sound;propext}
  For every natural number \(t\) and natural number \(k\) and natural number \(d\), if \(2 * t < twopow(k)\), then \(wittbitbool(wittscale(k, wittrow(t)), d + twopow(k + 1)) = wittbitbool(wittrow(t), d)\).
  \begin{proof}
  Close the goal with \(\lexeme{r30.witt::phiscale-row-testbit-sector-two-eq-row}(t, k, d)\).
  \end{proof}
  \end{theorem}

  \begin{theorem}{winwitt-testbit-sector-one-eq-row-xor}
  \allowaxioms{Classical.choice;Quot.sound;propext}
  For every natural number \(t\) and natural number \(k\) and natural number \(d\), if \(2 * t < twopow(k)\), then if \(d < twopow(k)\), then \(wittbitbool(wittwin(t, twopow(k)), d + twopow(k)) = bxor(wittbitbool(wittrow(t + twopow(k)), d + twopow(k)), wittbitbool(wittrow(t), d))\).
  \begin{proof}
  Close the goal with \(\lexeme{r30.witt::winwitt-testbit-sector-one-eq-row-xor}(t, k, d)\).
  \end{proof}
  \end{theorem}

  \begin{theorem}{winwitt-testbit-sector-two-eq-row-xor}
  \allowaxioms{Classical.choice;Quot.sound;propext}
  For every natural number \(t\) and natural number \(k\) and natural number \(d\), if \(2 * t < twopow(k)\), then \(wittbitbool(wittwin(t, twopow(k)), d + twopow(k + 1)) = bxor(wittbitbool(wittrow(t + twopow(k)), d + twopow(k + 1)), wittbitbool(wittrow(t), d))\).
  \begin{proof}
  Close the goal with \(\lexeme{r30.witt::winwitt-testbit-sector-two-eq-row-xor}(t, k, d)\).

  \end{theorem}

  \begin{theorem}{winwitt-sector-one-xor-sector-two-rhcenter-cone}
  \allowaxioms{Classical.choice;Quot.sound;propext}
  For every natural number \(t\) and natural number \(k\) and natural number \(p\), if \(t + p < twopow(k)\), then \(bxor(wittbitbool(wittwin(t, twopow(k)), (t + twopow(k)) - p), wittbitbool(wittwin(t, twopow(k)), (t + twopow(k + 1)) - p)) = bxor(rhcenter(t + twopow(k), p), lhcenter(t + twopow(k), twopow(k) - p))\).
  \begin{proof}
  Close the goal with \(\lexeme{r30.witt::winwitt-sector-one-xor-sector-two-rhcenter-cone}(t, k, p)\).
  \end{proof}
  \end{theorem}

  \begin{theorem}{winwitt-sector-one-xor-sector-two-lhcenter-cone}
  \allowaxioms{Classical.choice;Quot.sound;propext}
  For every natural number \(t\) and natural number \(k\) and natural number \(p\), if \(t + p < twopow(k)\), then \(bxor(wittbitbool(wittwin(t, twopow(k)), (t + p) + twopow(k)), wittbitbool(wittwin(t, twopow(k)), (t + p) + twopow(k + 1))) = bxor(lhcenter(t + twopow(k), p), lhcenter(t + twopow(k), p + twopow(k)))\).
  \begin{proof}
  Close the goal with \(\lexeme{r30.witt::winwitt-sector-one-xor-sector-two-lhcenter-cone}(t, k, p)\).
  \end{proof}
  \end{theorem}

  \begin{theorem}{lhbandagree-late-shift-of-winwittagree-sectors}
  \allowaxioms{Classical.choice;Quot.sound;propext}
  For every natural number \(t\) and natural number \(k\) and natural number \(Q\) and natural number \(p0\) and natural number \(L\), if \(((t + Q) + p0) + L < twopow(k)\), then if \(lhbandagree(t + twopow(k), Q, p0, L)\), then if \(winwittagree(t, k, Q, (t + p0) + twopow(k), L)\), then if \(winwittagree(t, k, Q, (t + p0) + twopow(k + 1), L)\), then \(lhbandagree(t + twopow(k), Q, p0 + twopow(k), L)\).
  \begin{proof}
  Close the goal with \(\lexeme{r30.witt::lhbandagree-late-shift-of-winwittagree-sectors}(t, k, Q, p0, L)\).
  \end{proof}
  \end{theorem}

  \begin{theorem}{rhbandagree-late-of-lhbandagree-late-and-winwittagree-sectors}
  \allowaxioms{Classical.choice;Quot.sound;propext}
  For every natural number \(t\) and natural number \(k\) and natural number \(Q\) and natural number \(L\), if \((t + Q) + L < twopow(k)\), then if \(lhbandagree(t + twopow(k), Q, twopow(k) - L, L)\), then if \(winwittagree(t, k, Q, (t + twopow(k)) - L, L)\), then if \(winwittagree(t, k, Q, (t + twopow(k + 1)) - L, L)\), then \(rhbandagree(t + twopow(k), Q, 0, L)\).
  \begin{proof}
  Close the goal with \(\lexeme{r30.witt::rhbandagree-late-of-lhbandagree-late-and-winwittagree-sectors}(t, k, Q, L)\).
  \end{proof}
  \end{theorem}

  \begin{theorem}{winwitt-testbit-twopow-eq-not-rhcenter-late}
  \allowaxioms{Classical.choice;Quot.sound;propext}
  For every natural number \(t\) and natural number \(k\), if \(2 * t < twopow(k)\), then \(wittbitbool(wittwin(t, twopow(k)), twopow(k)) = not(rhcenter(t + twopow(k), t))\).
  \begin{proof}
  Close the goal with \(\lexeme{r30.witt::winwitt-testbit-twopow-eq-not-rhcenter-late}(t, k)\).
  \end{proof}
  \end{theorem}

  \begin{theorem}{rhcenter-late-self-from-zero-gap-step}
  \allowaxioms{Classical.choice;Quot.sound;propext}
  For every natural number \(t\) and natural number \(k\), if \(2 * t + 2 < twopow(k)\), then \(rhcenter(t + twopow(k), t) = bxor(rhcenter((t + 1) + twopow(k), t + 1), rhcenter(t + twopow(k), t + 1) bor rhcenter(t + twopow(k), t + 2))\).
  \begin{proof}
  Close the goal with \(\lexeme{r30.witt::rhcenter-late-self-from-zero-gap-step}(t, k)\).
  \end{proof}
  \end{theorem}

  \begin{theorem}{lhcenter-late-shift-eq-ldiag}
  \allowaxioms{Classical.choice;Quot.sound;propext}
  For every natural number \(t\) and natural number \(k\) and natural number \(p\), if \(p ≤ t\), then \(lhcenter(t + twopow(k), p + twopow(k)) = ldiag(t - p, t + twopow(k))\).
  \begin{proof}
  Close the goal with \(\lexeme{r30.witt::lhcenter-late-shift-eq-ldiag}(t, k, p)\).
  \end{proof}
  \end{theorem}

  \begin{theorem}{lhcenter-late-cross-eq-ldiag}
  \allowaxioms{Classical.choice;Quot.sound;propext}
  For every natural number \(t\) and natural number \(k\) and natural number \(r\), if \(r ≤ twopow(k)\), then \(lhcenter(t + twopow(k), twopow(k) - r) = ldiag(t + r, t + twopow(k))\).
  \begin{proof}
  Close the goal with \(\lexeme{r30.witt::lhcenter-late-cross-eq-ldiag}(t, k, r)\).
  \end{proof}
  \end{theorem}

  \begin{theorem}{lhcenter-late-twopow-eq-zcenter}
  \allowaxioms{Classical.choice;Quot.sound;propext}
  For every natural number \(t\) and natural number \(k\), if \(t ≤ k\), then if \(settledat(t, k)\), then \(zcentereq(t, lhcenter(t + twopow(k), twopow(k)))\).
  \begin{proof}
  Close the goal with \(\lexeme{r30.witt::lhcenter-late-twopow-eq-zcenter}(t, k)\).
  \end{proof}
  \end{theorem}

  \allowaxioms{Classical.choice;Quot.sound;propext}
  For every natural number \(t\) and natural number \(k\), if \(t ≤ k\), then if \(settledat(t, k)\), then \(zcentereq(t, bxor(wittbitbool(wittwin(t, twopow(k)), t + twopow(k + 1)), center(t)))\).
